### FILE: README.md
================================================================================
# Helium - Маркетплейс услуг с системой рекомендаций

**Helium** - это соверменный маркетплейс, где заказчики размещают заказы на услуги (дизайн, программирование, ремонт, консультации), а исполнители откликаются на них. Встроенная система рекомендаций на основе правил и анализа поведения предлагает пользователям подходящие услуги и исполнителей.

![](https://github.com/DmProkofiev/HeliumDjangoProject/blob/main/static/img/CoverProject.jpeg)   

Архитектура **MVT (Model-View-Template)**.  
Динамические части (автодополнение, чаты, уведомления) работают через AJAX и WebSocket (Django Channels).

---

## Основные возможности

### Для незарегистрированных пользователей
- Просмотр каталога услуг с фильтрацией по категориям, цене, рейтингу.
- Просмотр профилей исполнителей.
- Поиск услуг с автодополнением.

### Для заказчиков
- Регистрация аккаунта, вход по email/логин и паролю.
- Создание заказа (название, описание, бюджет, сроки, категория).
- Управление заказами: редактирование, удаление, просмотр откликов.
- Выбор исполнителя и закрытие заказа.
- Оплата через систему **эскроу** (YooKassa) - средства перечисляются исполнителю после подтверждения выполнения.
- Оставление оценки и отзыва исполнителю.
- Персональные рекомендации услуг и исполнителей на главной странице и в личном кабинете.

### Для исполнителей
- Заполнение профиля: навыки, портфолио, цена за час/проект.
- Отклик на заказы с комментарием и своей ценой.
- Уведомления о новых откликах и изменении статуса заказа.
- Чат с заказчиком в реальном времени.
- Рекомендации подходящих заказов на основе навыков и истории.

### Административная панель
- Кастомизированный Django Admin для управления пользователями, заказами, категориями.
- Просмотр статистики: количество заказов, средний чек, активность пользователей.

---

## Технологический стек

| Компонент   | Технология                                  | Назначение |
|-------------|--------------------------------------------|------------|
| Backend     | Django (MVT)                               | ORM, админка, views, templates |
| База данных | PostgreSQL + `pg_trgm`                     | Хранение, полнотекстовый и нечёткий поиск |
| Real-time   | Django Channels (WebSocket)                | Чаты, уведомления |
| Платежи     | YooKassa (тестовый режим)                  | Эскроу-счета |
| FrontEnd    | Django Templates + Bootstrap + JavaScript  | SSR, AJAX, WebSocket |

> **Рекомендации** реализованы на основе машинного обучения: используются библиотека skit-learn
>
> **Поиск** реализован на PostgreSQL (триграммы + полнотекстовый), Elasticsearch не используется.

За основу и вдохновление взяты следующие известные маркетплейсы:
> https://aliexpress.ru/       
> https://www.ozon.ru/      
> https://www.wildberries.ru/       
> https://www.amazon.com/       
> https://www.alibabagroup.com/         

---

## Структура проекта Django

| Приложение        | Назначение                                          |
|-------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|
| `accounts`        | Пользователи, роли (заказчик/исполнитель), профили, |
| `core`            | Основа для содержания публикаций, лента             |
| `orders`          | Заказы, отклики, статусы, коммерческие сделки       |
| `chat`            | Чаты и уведомления в реальном времени               |
| `recommendations` | Формирование персональных рекомендаций              |
| `payments`        | Интеграция с YooKassa (эскроу)                      |

## pip list
- Python 3.x
- Django
- Daphne (ASGI-сервер)
- Pillow (работа с изображениями)
- python-dotenv (загрузка переменных окружения)
- yookassa()
---

daphne -b 127.0.0.1 -p 8000 main.asgi:application

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'main.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


================================================================================
### FILE: project_dump.py
================================================================================
import os
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).parent
OUT = ROOT / "project_dump.txt"

# включения
INCLUDE_EXT = {
    # backend
    ".py",
    # frontend
    ".js", ".jsx", ".mjs", ".ts", ".tsx",
    ".css", ".scss", ".sass",
    # разметка и данные
    ".html", ".htm", ".jinja", ".jinja2",
    ".json", ".yml", ".yaml", ".toml",
    ".cfg", ".ini", ".conf", ".txt", ".md",
    # прочее
    ".env.example", ".sql",
}
INCLUDE_NAMES = {
    "Dockerfile", "docker-compose.yml", "docker-compose.yaml",
    "requirements.txt", "manage.py", "README.md", "README",
    "nginx.conf", "Procfile", "Makefile", ".env.example",
    "package.json", "package-lock.json",
}

# исключения
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".git", ".github", ".venv", "venv", "env",
    "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", ".mypy_cache", ".ruff_cache",
    "node_modules", "staticfiles", "media", "dist", "build",
    ".idea", ".vscode", "migrations",
}
EXCLUDE_FILES = {"db.sqlite3", "project_dump.txt", ".env", "project_tree.txt"}


def should_include(path: Path) -> bool:
    if path.name in EXCLUDE_FILES:
        return False
    if any(part in EXCLUDE_DIRS for part in path.relative_to(ROOT).parts):
        return False
    if path.name in INCLUDE_NAMES:
        return True
    return path.suffix.lower() in INCLUDE_EXT


def build_tree(root: Path) -> str:
    lines = [f"{root.name}/"]

    def walk(dir_path: Path, prefix: str = ""):
        try:
            entries = sorted(
                [e for e in dir_path.iterdir()
                 if e.name not in EXCLUDE_DIRS and e.name not in EXCLUDE_FILES],
                key=lambda p: (p.is_file(), p.name.lower()),
            )
        except PermissionError:
            return
        for i, entry in enumerate(entries):
            last = i == len(entries) - 1
            connector = "└── " if last else "├── "
            lines.append(prefix + connector + entry.name + ("/" if entry.is_dir() else ""))
            if entry.is_dir():
                walk(entry, prefix + ("    " if last else "│   "))

    walk(root)
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main():
    parts = []
    parts.append("=" * 80)
    parts.append("PROJECT TREE")
    parts.append("=" * 80)
    parts.append(build_tree(ROOT))
    parts.append("")

    total = 0
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(ROOT):
        dirnames[:] = sorted(d for d in dirnames if d not in EXCLUDE_DIRS)
        for fname in sorted(filenames):
            fpath = Path(dirpath) / fname
            if not should_include(fpath):
                continue
            rel = fpath.relative_to(ROOT)
            try:
                content = fpath.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                continue
            parts.append(f"\n{'=' * 80}\n### FILE: {rel}\n{'=' * 80}\n")
            parts.append(content)
            parts.append("\n")
            total += 1

    OUT.write_text("".join(parts), encoding="utf-8")
    size_kb = OUT.stat().st_size // 1024
    print(f"Готово: {OUT} — {total} файлов, {size_kb} KB")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth.admin import UserAdmin as DjangoUserAdmin

from .models import User


@admin.register(User)
class UserAdmin(DjangoUserAdmin):
    fieldsets = DjangoUserAdmin.fieldsets + (
        ('Дополнительная информация', {
            'fields': ('phone', 'city', 'avatar', 'role'),
        }),
    )
    list_display = ('username', 'email', 'role', 'phone', 'city', 'avatar', 'is_staff', 'is_active')
    list_filter = DjangoUserAdmin.list_filter + ('role',)

### FILE: accounts\administrator_service.py
================================================================================
import logging

from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q

from core import publication_service
from core.models import Publication
from .models import User
from .permissions import is_admin_user

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ВНУТРЕННИЕ
def _ensure_admin(actor):
    if not is_admin_user(actor):
        raise PermissionDenied('Доступ только для администратора')

# ПОЛЬЗОВАТЕЛИ
def list_users(query=None):
    qs = User.objects.all().order_by('-date_joined')
    if query:
        qs = qs.filter(
            Q(username__icontains=query)
            | Q(email__icontains=query)
            | Q(phone__icontains=query)
        )
    return qs


def toggle_user_block(actor, target_id):
    _ensure_admin(actor)

    target = User.objects.get(id=target_id)
    if target.id == actor.id:
        raise ValueError('Нельзя заблокировать собственный аккаунт')
    if target.is_superuser:
        raise ValueError('Нельзя заблокировать суперпользователя')

    target.is_active = not target.is_active
    target.save(update_fields=['is_active'])
    logger.info(
        'Admin %s %s user %s',
        actor.id,
        'blocked' if not target.is_active else 'unblocked',
        target.id,
    )
    return target

# ПУБЛИКАЦИИ
def list_publications(query=None, status=None):
    qs = (
        Publication.objects
        .select_related('author', 'category')
        .order_by('-created_at')
    )
    if query:
        qs = qs.filter(
            Q(title__icontains=query)
            | Q(author__username__icontains=query)
        )
    allowed_statuses = {'active', 'frozen', 'in_progress', 'closed', 'archived'}
    if status in allowed_statuses:
        qs = qs.filter(status=status)
    return qs

def freeze_publication(actor, slug):
    _ensure_admin(actor)
    publication = Publication.objects.get(slug=slug)
    if publication.status == 'frozen':
        return publication, False
    publication.status = 'frozen'
    publication.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
    logger.info('Admin %s froze publication %s', actor.id, publication.id)
    return publication, True

def unfreeze_publication(actor, slug):
    _ensure_admin(actor)
    publication = Publication.objects.get(slug=slug)
    if publication.status != 'frozen':
        return publication, False

    publication.status = 'active'
    publication.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
    logger.info('Admin %s unfroze publication %s', actor.id, publication.id)
    return publication, True

def delete_publication(actor, slug):
    _ensure_admin(actor)
    publication = Publication.objects.get(slug=slug)
    title = publication.title
    publication_id = publication.id
    publication_service.delete_publication(publication)
    logger.info('Admin %s deleted publication %s', actor.id, publication_id)
    return title

def get_publication_for_admin_edit(actor, slug):
    _ensure_admin(actor)
    return Publication.objects.get(slug=slug)

@transaction.atomic
def update_publication(actor, publication, form, files=None):
    _ensure_admin(actor)
    result = publication_service.update_publication(publication, form, files)
    logger.info('Admin %s updated publication %s', actor.id, publication.id)
    return result

from django import forms
from django.contrib.auth.forms import UserCreationForm, AuthenticationForm
from .models import User

class UserRegistrationForm(UserCreationForm):
    email = forms.EmailField(
        required=True,
        widget=forms.EmailInput(attrs={
            'class': 'form-control',
            'placeholder': 'Email'
        }),
        label='Email'
    )

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ('username', 'email', 'phone', 'city', 'avatar', 'password1', 'password2')
        widgets = {
            'username': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'placeholder': 'Имя пользователя'}),
            'phone': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'placeholder': '+7 (___) ___-__-__'}),
            'city': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'placeholder': 'Город'})
        }

    def clean_email(self):
        email = self.cleaned_data.get('email')
        if User.objects.filter(email=email).exists():
            raise forms.ValidationError('Пользователь с таким email уже зарегистрирован.')
        return email

class UserUpdateForm(forms.ModelForm):
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ('username', 'email', 'phone', 'city', 'avatar')
        widgets = {
            'username': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'email': forms.EmailInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'phone': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'city': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'})
        }

    def clean_email(self):
        email = self.cleaned_data.get('email')
        if User.objects.filter(email=email).exclude(pk=self.instance.pk).exists():
            raise forms.ValidationError('Этот email уже занят.')
        return email

class UserLoginForm(AuthenticationForm):
    username = forms.CharField(widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'placeholder': 'Имя пользователя'}), label='Имя пользователя')
    password = forms.CharField(widget=forms.PasswordInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'placeholder': 'Пароль'}), label='Пароль')

### FILE: accounts\models.py
================================================================================
from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractUser
from django.db import models

# Класс пользавателя
class User(AbstractUser):
    ROLE = (
        ('user', 'Пользаватель'),
        ('admin', 'Администратор')
    )
    email = models.EmailField(unique=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, null=True)
    city = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name='Город')
    role = models.CharField(max_length=10,choices=ROLE,default='user',verbose_name='Роль')
    avatar = models.ImageField('аватар', upload_to='avatars/', blank=True, null=True)
    is_verified = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    groups = models.ManyToManyField('auth.Group', related_name='custom_user_groups', blank=True, verbose_name='Группы')
    user_permissions = models.ManyToManyField('auth.Permission',related_name='custom_user_permissions',blank=True,verbose_name='Права')

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Пользователь'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Пользователи'

    def __str__(self):
        return self.username

# Избранное
class Favorite(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(User,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='favorites', verbose_name='Пользователь')
    publication = models.ForeignKey('core.Publication',on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='favorited_by',verbose_name='Публикация')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Добавлено', auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Избранное'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Избранное'
        unique_together = ('user', 'publication')
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.user} → {self.publication}'

# Корзина
class Cart(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(User,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='cart_items',verbose_name='Пользователь')
    publication = models.ForeignKey('core.Publication',on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='in_carts',verbose_name='Публикация')
    quantity = models.PositiveIntegerField('Количество', default=1)
    added_at = models.DateTimeField('Добавлено', auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Корзина'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Корзина'
        unique_together = ('user', 'publication')
        ordering = ['-added_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.user} → {self.publication} ({self.quantity})'

    @property
    def total(self):
        return self.publication.price * self.quantity

# Способ оплаты
class PaymentMethod(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='payment_methods', verbose_name='Пользователь')
    payment_method_id = models.CharField('ID карты в YooKassa', max_length=100, unique=True, null=True, blank=True)
    title = models.CharField('Название', max_length=100, blank=True)
    card_last4 = models.CharField('Последние 4 цифры', max_length=4, blank=True)
    card_type = models.CharField('Тип карты', max_length=20, blank=True)
    is_default = models.BooleanField('По умолчанию', default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Добавлена', auto_now_add=True)
    is_verified = models.BooleanField('Проверена', default=True)
    is_pending = models.BooleanField('В процессе привязки', default=False)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Способ оплаты'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Способы оплаты'
        ordering = ['-is_default', '-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title or f'•••• {self.card_last4}'

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.is_default:
            PaymentMethod.objects.filter(user=self.user).exclude(pk=self.pk).update(is_default=False)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

### FILE: accounts\pay_service.py

import uuid
import logging

from django.conf import settings
from django.http import HttpResponse, HttpResponseBadRequest
from yookassa import Configuration, Payment as YooPayment, Refund

from .models import PaymentMethod

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

Configuration.account_id = settings.YOOKASSA_SHOP_ID
Configuration.secret_key = settings.YOOKASSA_SECRET_KEY


def create_binding_payment(method, return_url):
    """
    Создаёт в YooKassa платёж на 1 рубль с save_payment_method=True.
    capture=True — деньги списываются сразу; после успешной привязки
    мы возвращаем 1 ₽ через refund_binding_payment.
    """
    payment = YooPayment.create({
        'amount': {'value': '1.00', 'currency': 'RUB'},
        'capture': True,
        'save_payment_method': True,
        'confirmation': {
            'type': 'redirect',
            'return_url': return_url,
        },
        'description': 'Привязка карты',
        'metadata': {
            'method_id': method.id,
            'user_id': method.user_id,
        },
    }, str(uuid.uuid4()))

    return {
        'payment_id': payment.id,
        'confirmation_url': payment.confirmation.confirmation_url,
    }


def refund_binding_payment(payment_id):
    """
    Возвращает 1 рубль после успешной привязки карты.

    Идемпотентность: ключ строится от payment_id, поэтому повторный
    вызов (например, вебхук + возврат с YooKassa) не создаст второй
    возврат — YooKassa вернёт тот же результат.
    """
    return Refund.create({
        'payment_id': payment_id,
        'amount': {'value': '1.00', 'currency': 'RUB'},
    }, f'refund-binding-{payment_id}')


def find_payment(payment_id):
    """Запрашивает платёж в YooKassa для сверки статуса."""
    return YooPayment.find_one(payment_id)


def sync_binding_from_remote(method, real_payment):
    """
    Заполняет PaymentMethod данными карты из объекта YooKassa Payment.

    Возвращает True, только если:
    - платёж в статусе 'succeeded';
    - в платеже есть сохранённый payment_method.

    В противном случае — False, метод остаётся is_pending=True,
    и пользователь/вебхук могут повторить попытку позже.
    """
    remote_status = getattr(real_payment, 'status', None)
    if remote_status != 'succeeded':
        logger.info(
            'sync_binding_from_remote: платёж %s в статусе %s, ждём succeeded',
            getattr(real_payment, 'id', '?'), remote_status,
        )
        return False

    payment_method_data = getattr(real_payment, 'payment_method', None)
    if not payment_method_data or not getattr(payment_method_data, 'saved', False):
        logger.info(
            'sync_binding_from_remote: платёж %s не содержит сохранённой карты',
            getattr(real_payment, 'id', '?'),
        )
        return False

    card = getattr(payment_method_data, 'card', None)
    card_last4 = getattr(card, 'last4', '') or ''
    card_type = getattr(card, 'card_type', '') or ''

    method.payment_method_id = payment_method_data.id
    method.card_last4 = card_last4
    method.card_type = card_type
    method.title = f'{card_type or "Карта"} •••• {card_last4}'
    method.is_pending = False
    method.save()

    # Первая подтверждённая карта автоматически становится основной.
    if not method.user.payment_methods.filter(is_pending=False).exclude(pk=method.pk).exists():
        method.is_default = True
        method.save(update_fields=['is_default'])

    logger.info(
        'Карта привязана для user_id=%s (method #%s, %s •••• %s)',
        method.user_id, method.id, card_type or 'Карта', card_last4 or '????',
    )
    return True


def handle_binding_webhook(data):
    """
    Обрабатывает вебхук привязки карты.
    Требует:
    - event == payment.succeeded;
    - metadata.method_id указывает на существующий PaymentMethod в is_pending=True;
    - запрос пришёл с IP YooKassa (проверяется в views);
    - платёж принадлежит нашему магазину.
    """
    event = data.get('event')
    payment_obj = data.get('object', {})
    yookassa_payment_id = payment_obj.get('id')
    method_id = payment_obj.get('metadata', {}).get('method_id')

    if not method_id or not yookassa_payment_id:
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    method = PaymentMethod.objects.filter(id=method_id, is_pending=True).first()
    if not method:
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    try:
        real_payment = find_payment(yookassa_payment_id)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception('Binding: payment %s not found', yookassa_payment_id)
        return HttpResponseBadRequest('Payment not found')

    recipient = getattr(real_payment, 'recipient', None)
    if recipient:
        account_id = getattr(recipient, 'account_id', None)
        if account_id and account_id != settings.YOOKASSA_SHOP_ID:
            logger.error('Binding: recipient mismatch for method #%s', method_id)
            return HttpResponseBadRequest('Recipient mismatch')

    if event != 'payment.succeeded':
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    if not sync_binding_from_remote(method, real_payment):
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    # Возврат 1 ₽ — best effort; повторный вызов идемпотентен.
    try:
        refund_binding_payment(yookassa_payment_id)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception('Binding: refund failed for payment %s', yookassa_payment_id)

    return HttpResponse(status=200)

### FILE: accounts\permissions.py

from functools import wraps
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied

def is_admin_user(user):
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        return False
    return bool(
        user.is_staff
        or user.is_superuser
        or getattr(user, 'role', None) == 'admin'
    )

def admin_required(view_func):
    @wraps(view_func)
    @login_required
    def _wrapped(request, *args, **kwargs):
        if not is_admin_user(request.user):
            raise PermissionDenied('Доступ только для администратора')
        return view_func(request, *args, **kwargs)
    return _wrapped

import json
import logging
import time

from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth import login, logout, get_user_model
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.db import IntegrityError
from django.db.models import Q
from django.http import JsonResponse, HttpResponseBadRequest, HttpResponseForbidden
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect, get_object_or_404
from django.urls import reverse
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST

from core.forms import PublicationForm
from core.models import Publication
from orders.models import Order
from orders.security import is_yookassa_request
from recommendations.logging_service import log_interaction

from . import administrator_service
from .forms import UserRegistrationForm, UserUpdateForm, UserLoginForm
from .models import Favorite, Cart, User, PaymentMethod
from .pay_service import (
    create_binding_payment,
    handle_binding_webhook,
    find_payment,
    refund_binding_payment,
    sync_binding_from_remote,
)
from .permissions import admin_required, is_admin_user

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# АУТЕНТИФИКАЦИЯ
def login_view(request):
    """Вход по логину и паролю; редирект на next или на ленту."""
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect('core:feed')

    form = UserLoginForm(request, data=request.POST or None)
    if request.method == 'POST' and form.is_valid():
        user = form.get_user()
        login(request, user)
        next_url = request.GET.get('next') or request.POST.get('next')
        return redirect(next_url or 'core:feed')

    return render(request, 'accounts/login.html', {'form': form})


def logout_view(request):
    """Выход и редирект на ленту."""
    logout(request)
    return redirect('core:feed')


def register_view(request):
    """Регистрация пользователя; после успеха — автологин."""
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect('core:feed')

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = UserRegistrationForm(request.POST, request.FILES)
        if form.is_valid():
            user = form.save()
            login(request, user)
            return redirect('core:feed')
    else:
        form = UserRegistrationForm()

    return render(request, 'accounts/register.html', {'form': form})


# ПРОФИЛЬ

@login_required
def profile_view(request):
    """
    Личный кабинет: публикации, заказы, избранное, корзина, оплата.
    Для администратора добавляет данные для панелей
    «Пользователи» и «Все публикации».
    """
    user = request.user
    is_admin = is_admin_user(user)

    orders = (
        Order.objects
        .filter(Q(customer=user) | Q(executor=user))
        .select_related('customer', 'executor', 'publication', 'payment')
        .order_by('-created_at')
    )

    context = {
        'my_publications': Publication.objects
            .filter(author=user)
            .order_by('-created_at'),
        'favorites': Favorite.objects
            .filter(user=user)
            .select_related('publication', 'publication__author', 'publication__category'),
        'cart_items': Cart.objects
            .filter(user=user)
            .select_related('publication', 'publication__author', 'publication__category'),
        'payment_methods': user.payment_methods.all(),
        'orders': orders,
        'is_admin': is_admin,
    }

    if is_admin:
        admin_search = request.GET.get('admin_search', '').strip()
        admin_status = request.GET.get('admin_status', '').strip()

        admin_users_qs = administrator_service.list_users(admin_search)
        admin_pubs_qs = administrator_service.list_publications(admin_search, admin_status)

        context.update({
            'admin_users': admin_users_qs,
            'admin_users_count': admin_users_qs.count(),
            'admin_publications': admin_pubs_qs,
            'admin_publications_count': admin_pubs_qs.count(),
            'admin_search': admin_search,
            'admin_status': admin_status,
        })

    return render(request, 'accounts/account_detail.html', context)


@login_required
def profile_edit_view(request):
    """Редактирование профиля пользователя."""
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = UserUpdateForm(request.POST, request.FILES, instance=request.user)
        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()
            return redirect('accounts:account_detail')
    else:
        form = UserUpdateForm(instance=request.user)

    return render(request, 'accounts/profile_edit.html', {'form': form})


def account_user_detail_view(request, username):
    """Публичный профиль пользователя с активными публикациями."""
    UserModel = get_user_model()
    author = get_object_or_404(UserModel, username=username)
    publications = (
        Publication.objects
        .filter(author=author, status='active')
        .select_related('category')
        .order_by('-created_at')
    )
    return render(request, 'accounts/account_user_detail.html', {
        'author': author,
        'publications': publications,
    })


# ЛАЙКИ И КОРЗИНА

@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle_like(request, publication_id):
    """Переключает лайк публикации."""
    publication = get_object_or_404(Publication, id=publication_id)
    like, created = Favorite.objects.get_or_create(
        user=request.user, publication=publication,
    )
    if not created:
        like.delete()
    else:
        try:
            log_interaction(request, 'like', publication=publication)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception('log_interaction failed on like')

    likes_count = Favorite.objects.filter(publication=publication).count()

    if request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest':
        return JsonResponse({'liked': created, 'likes_count': likes_count})

    return redirect(request.META.get('HTTP_REFERER', 'core:feed'))


@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle_cart(request, publication_id):
    """Переключает наличие публикации в корзине."""
    publication = get_object_or_404(Publication, id=publication_id)
    in_cart = False

    try:
        Cart.objects.create(user=request.user, publication=publication)
        in_cart = True
        try:
            log_interaction(request, 'cart', publication=publication)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception('log_interaction failed on cart')
    except IntegrityError:
        Cart.objects.filter(user=request.user, publication=publication).delete()
        in_cart = False

    if request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest':
        return JsonResponse({
            'in_cart': in_cart,
            'cart_count': Cart.objects.filter(user=request.user).count(),
        })

    return redirect(request.META.get('HTTP_REFERER') or 'core:feed')


# СПОСОБЫ ОПЛАТЫ

@login_required
@require_POST
def payment_method_add_view(request):
    """Создаёт PaymentMethod в is_pending=True и инициирует привязку."""
    method = PaymentMethod.objects.create(user=request.user, is_pending=True)
    return_url = request.build_absolute_uri(
        reverse(
            'accounts:payment_method_binding_return',
            kwargs={'method_id': method.id},
        )
    )
    try:
        result = create_binding_payment(method, return_url)
    except Exception as exc:
        method.delete()
        messages.error(request, f'Не удалось начать привязку: {exc}')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')

    method.payment_method_id = result['payment_id']
    method.save(update_fields=['payment_method_id'])
    return redirect(result['confirmation_url'])


def _try_sync_binding(method, attempts=3, delay=1.0):
    """Пробует синхронизировать привязку несколько раз подряд."""
    for i in range(attempts):
        try:
            real = find_payment(method.payment_method_id)
            if sync_binding_from_remote(method, real):
                return True
        except Exception:
            logger.exception('sync attempt %d failed for method #%s', i, method.id)
        if i < attempts - 1:
            time.sleep(delay)
    return False


@login_required
def payment_method_binding_return_view(request, method_id):
    """Возврат из YooKassa после привязки карты."""
    method = get_object_or_404(PaymentMethod, id=method_id, user=request.user)

    if method.is_pending and method.payment_method_id:
        synced = _try_sync_binding(method, attempts=3, delay=1.0)
        if synced:
            try:
                refund_binding_payment(method.payment_method_id)
            except Exception:
                logger.exception('Возврат 1 ₽ не удался для method #%s', method.id)
            messages.success(request, 'Карта успешно привязана')
        else:
            messages.info(
                request,
                'YooKassa ещё обрабатывает привязку. '
                'Нажмите «Проверить статус» через минуту.',
            )

    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')


@login_required
@require_POST
def payment_method_check_binding_view(request, method_id):
    """Ручная повторная синхронизация pending-карты."""
    method = get_object_or_404(PaymentMethod, id=method_id, user=request.user)

    if not method.is_pending:
        messages.info(request, 'Карта уже привязана')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')

    if not method.payment_method_id:
        messages.error(request, 'У карты отсутствует идентификатор YooKassa. Удалите и добавьте заново.')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')

    try:
        real = find_payment(method.payment_method_id)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception('Check binding: cannot fetch payment for method #%s', method.id)
        messages.error(request, 'Не удалось запросить YooKassa. Попробуйте позже.')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')

    remote_status = getattr(real, 'status', None)

    if remote_status == 'succeeded' and sync_binding_from_remote(method, real):
        try:
            refund_binding_payment(method.payment_method_id)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception('Check binding: refund failed for method #%s', method.id)
        messages.success(request, 'Карта успешно привязана')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')

    if remote_status == 'canceled':
        method.delete()
        messages.error(request, 'Привязка карты была отменена или не удалась. Карта удалена.')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')

    messages.warning(
        request,
        f'YooKassa ещё не подтвердила карту (статус: {remote_status or "неизвестен"}). '
        f'Попробуйте снова через минуту.',
    )
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')


@login_required
@require_POST
def payment_method_edit_view(request, method_id):
    """Изменение отображаемого названия карты."""
    method = get_object_or_404(PaymentMethod, id=method_id, user=request.user)
    title = request.POST.get('title', '').strip()
    if len(title) <= 100:
        method.title = title
        method.save(update_fields=['title'])
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')


@login_required
@require_POST
def payment_method_remove_view(request, method_id):
    """Удаление сохранённого способа оплаты."""
    method = get_object_or_404(PaymentMethod, id=method_id, user=request.user)
    method.delete()
    messages.success(request, 'Способ оплаты удалён')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')


@login_required
@require_POST
def payment_method_default_view(request, method_id):
    """Установка способа оплаты по умолчанию."""
    method = get_object_or_404(PaymentMethod, id=method_id, user=request.user)
    method.is_default = True
    method.save()
    messages.success(request, 'Карта выбрана по умолчанию')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=payments')


# ВЕБХУК YOOKASSA ДЛЯ ПРИВЯЗКИ
@csrf_exempt
@require_POST
def yookassa_binding_webhook_view(request):
    """Вебхук для привязки карт с обязательной проверкой IP"""
    if not is_yookassa_request(request):
        logger.warning('Binding webhook: forbidden source')
        return HttpResponseForbidden('Forbidden')
    try:
        data = json.loads(request.body)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return HttpResponseBadRequest('Invalid JSON')
    return handle_binding_webhook(data)

# АДМИНИСТРИРОВАНИЕ: ПОЛЬЗОВАТЕЛИ
@admin_required
@require_POST
def admin_toggle_user_block_view(request, user_id):
    try:
        target = administrator_service.toggle_user_block(request.user, user_id)
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        messages.error(request, 'Пользователь не найден')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-users')
    except ValueError as exc:
        messages.error(request, str(exc))
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-users')

    action = 'разблокирован' if target.is_active else 'заблокирован'
    messages.success(request, f'Пользователь «{target.username}» {action}')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-users')

# АДМИНИСТРИРОВАНИЕ: ПУБЛИКАЦИИ
@admin_required
@require_POST
def admin_freeze_publication_view(request, slug):
    try:
        publication, changed = administrator_service.freeze_publication(request.user, slug)
    except Publication.DoesNotExist:
        messages.error(request, 'Публикация не найдена')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')

    if changed:
        messages.success(request, f'Публикация «{publication.title}» заморожена')
    else:
        messages.info(request, 'Публикация уже заморожена')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')


@admin_required
@require_POST
def admin_unfreeze_publication_view(request, slug):
    try:
        publication, changed = administrator_service.unfreeze_publication(request.user, slug)
    except Publication.DoesNotExist:
        messages.error(request, 'Публикация не найдена')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')

    if changed:
        messages.success(request, f'Публикация «{publication.title}» разморожена')
    else:
        messages.info(request, 'Публикация не была заморожена')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')

@admin_required
@require_POST
def admin_delete_publication_view(request, slug):
    try:
        title = administrator_service.delete_publication(request.user, slug)
    except Publication.DoesNotExist:
        messages.error(request, 'Публикация не найдена')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')

    messages.success(request, f'Публикация «{title}» удалена')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')

@admin_required
def admin_publication_update_view(request, slug):
    try:
        publication = administrator_service.get_publication_for_admin_edit(
            request.user, slug,
        )
    except Publication.DoesNotExist:
        messages.error(request, 'Публикация не найдена')
        return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = PublicationForm(request.POST, request.FILES, instance=publication)
        files = request.FILES.getlist('images_upload')
        if form.is_valid():
            administrator_service.update_publication(
                request.user, publication, form, files,
            )
            messages.success(request, 'Изменения сохранены')
            return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=admin-publications')
    else:
        form = PublicationForm(instance=publication)

    return render(request, 'core/publication_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'publication': publication,
        'is_edit': True,
        'is_admin_edit': True,
    })

================================================================================
### FILE: chats\consumers.py
================================================================================
import json
from channels.db import database_sync_to_async
from channels.generic.websocket import AsyncWebsocketConsumer
from django.db.models import Q
from .models import Chat, Message

class ChatConsumer(AsyncWebsocketConsumer):
    async def connect(self):
        self.user = self.scope['user']
        self.chat_id = self.scope['url_route']['kwargs']['chat_id']
        self.room_group_name = f'chat_{self.chat_id}'
        if not self.user.is_authenticated:
            await self.close()
            return
        if not await self.is_participant():
            await self.close()
            return
        await self.channel_layer.group_add(self.room_group_name, self.channel_name)
        await self.accept()

    async def disconnect(self, close_code):
        if hasattr(self, 'room_group_name'):
            await self.channel_layer.group_discard(self.room_group_name, self.channel_name)

    async def receive(self, text_data):
        try:
            data = json.loads(text_data)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return
        text = (data.get('text') or '').strip()
        if not text:
            return
        if len(text) > 2000:
            text = text[:2000]
        message = await self.save_message(text)
        await self.channel_layer.group_send(
            self.room_group_name,
            {
                'type': 'chat_message',
                'message_id': message['id'],
                'text': message['text'],
                'author_id': message['author_id'],
                'author': message['author'],
                'created_at': message['created_at'],
            }
        )

    async def chat_message(self, event):
        await self.send(text_data=json.dumps({
            'message_id': event['message_id'],
            'text': event['text'],
            'author_id': event['author_id'],
            'author': event['author'],
            'created_at': event['created_at'],
        }))

    @database_sync_to_async
    def is_participant(self):
        return Chat.objects.filter(id=self.chat_id).filter(
            Q(user1=self.user) | Q(user2=self.user)
        ).exists()

    @database_sync_to_async
    def save_message(self, text):
        chat = Chat.objects.get(id=self.chat_id)
        message = Message.objects.create(chat=chat, author=self.user, text=text)
        chat.save(update_fields=['updated_at'])
        return {
            'id': message.id,
            'text': message.text,
            'author_id': message.author_id,
            'author': message.author.username,
            'created_at': message.created_at.isoformat(),
        }

### FILE: chats\models.py

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import IntegrityError, models, transaction


class Chat(models.Model):
    user1 = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='chats_as_user1',verbose_name='Участник 1')
    user2 = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='chats_as_user2',verbose_name='Участник 2')
    publication = models.ForeignKey('core.Publication',on_delete=models.SET_NULL,null=True,blank=True,related_name='chats',verbose_name='Публикация')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Создан', auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField('Обновлён', auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Диалог'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Диалоги'
        ordering = ['-updated_at']
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=['user1', 'user2', 'publication'],
                condition=models.Q(publication__isnull=False),
                name='unique_chat_with_publication',
            ),
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=['user1', 'user2'],
                condition=models.Q(publication__isnull=True),
                name='unique_chat_without_publication',
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Диалог {self.pk}: {self.user1} | {self.user2}'

    def has_participant(self, user):
        return user.is_authenticated and user.id in (self.user1_id, self.user2_id)

    def get_other_user(self, user):
        if user.id == self.user1_id:
            return self.user2
        if user.id == self.user2_id:
            return self.user1
        return None

    def last_message(self):
        return self.messages.order_by('-created_at').first()

    def unread_count_for(self, user):
        return self.messages.filter(is_read=False).exclude(author=user).count()

    @classmethod
    def get_or_create_between(cls, user_a, user_b, publication=None):
        if user_a.id == user_b.id:
            raise ValueError('Нельзя создать Диалог с самим собой.')

        u1, u2 = (user_a, user_b) if user_a.id < user_b.id else (user_b, user_a)

        try:
            chat = cls.objects.get(user1=u1, user2=u2, publication=publication)
            return chat, False
        except cls.DoesNotExist:
            pass

        try:
            with transaction.atomic():
                chat = cls.objects.create(user1=u1, user2=u2, publication=publication)
            return chat, True
        except IntegrityError:
            chat = cls.objects.get(user1=u1, user2=u2, publication=publication)
            return chat, False


class Message(models.Model):
    chat = models.ForeignKey(Chat, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='messages', verbose_name='Диалог')
    author = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='messages', verbose_name='Автор')
    text = models.TextField('Текст')
    is_read = models.BooleanField('Прочитано', default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Отправлено', auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Сообщение'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Сообщения'
        ordering = ['created_at']
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=['chat', 'created_at'])]

    def __str__(self):
        return f'{self.author}: {self.text[:30]}'

from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied
from django.db.models import Q
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from accounts.models import User
from core.models import Publication
from .models import Chat, Message

# READ
@login_required
def chat_list_view(request):
    chats = Chat.objects.filter(Q(user1=request.user) | Q(user2=request.user)).select_related('user1', 'user2', 'publication').order_by('-updated_at')
    chats_data = [
        {
            'chat': chat,
            'other': chat.get_other_user(request.user),
            'last_message': chat.last_message(),
            'unread': chat.unread_count_for(request.user),
        }
        for chat in chats
    ]
    return render(request, 'chats/chat_list.html', {'chats_data': chats_data})


@login_required
def chat_detail_view(request, chat_id):
    chat = get_object_or_404(Chat, id=chat_id)
    if not chat.has_participant(request.user):
        raise PermissionDenied('Вы не являетесь участником этого чата')
    chat.messages.filter(is_read=False).exclude(author=request.user).update(is_read=True)
    return render(request, 'chats/chat_detail.html', {
        'chat': chat,
        'chat_messages': chat.messages.select_related('author'),
        'other_user': chat.get_other_user(request.user)
    })

# CREATE
@login_required
@require_POST
def create_chat_from_publication_view(request, publication_id):
    publication = get_object_or_404(Publication, id=publication_id)
    if publication.author_id == request.user.id:
        return redirect('core:publication_detail', slug=publication.slug)
    chat, _ = Chat.get_or_create_between(user_a=request.user, user_b=publication.author, publication=publication)
    return redirect('chats:chat_detail', chat_id=chat.id)

@login_required
@require_POST
def create_free_chat_view(request, user_id):
    other = get_object_or_404(User, id=user_id)
    if other.id == request.user.id:
        return redirect('accounts:profile')
    chat, _ = Chat.get_or_create_between(request.user, other)
    return redirect('chats:chat_detail', chat_id=chat.id)

# UPDATE
@login_required
@require_POST
def update_message_view(request, message_id):
    message = get_object_or_404(Message, id=message_id)
    if message.author_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Нельзя редактировать чужое сообщение.')
    if not message.chat.has_participant(request.user):
        raise PermissionDenied('Вы не участник этого чата')
    text = request.POST.get('text', '').strip()
    if text and len(text) <= 2000:
        message.text = text
        message.save(update_fields=['text'])
    return redirect('chats:chat_detail', chat_id=message.chat_id)

# DELETE
@login_required
@require_POST
def remove_message_view(request, message_id):
    message = get_object_or_404(Message, id=message_id)
    if message.author_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Нельзя удалить чужое сообщение')
    chat_id = message.chat_id
    message.delete()
    return redirect('chats:chat_detail', chat_id=chat_id)

@login_required
@require_POST
def remove_chat_view(request, chat_id):
    chat = get_object_or_404(Chat, id=chat_id)
    if not chat.has_participant(request.user):
        raise PermissionDenied('Вы не участник этого диалога')
    chat.delete()
    return redirect('chats:list')

================================================================================
### FILE: comments\comment_service.py
================================================================================
import os
import shutil
import uuid
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied, ValidationError
from django.db import transaction
from .models import Comments

ALLOWED = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp'}
MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMAGES = 5


# ============================================================
# CREATE
# ============================================================
@transaction.atomic
def create_comment(form, user, publication, files=None):
    comment = form.save(commit=False)
    comment.author = user
    comment.publication = publication
    comment.image = []
    comment.save()

    if files:
        _append_images(comment, files)
    return comment


# ============================================================
# UPDATE
# ============================================================
@transaction.atomic
def update_comment(comment, user, text, files=None):
    if comment.author_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш комментарий')

    comment.text = text
    comment.save(update_fields=['text'])

    if files:
        _append_images(comment, files)
    return comment


# ============================================================
# DELETE
# ============================================================
@transaction.atomic
def delete_comment(comment, user):
    if comment.author_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш комментарий')

    folder = _folder(comment)
    comment.delete()
    if os.path.isdir(folder):
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)


# ============================================================
# DELETE IMAGE
# ============================================================
@transaction.atomic
def delete_comment_image(comment, index, user):
    if comment.author_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш комментарий')

    names = list(comment.image_list)
    if index < 0 or index >= len(names):
        return

    name = os.path.basename(names.pop(index))
    path = os.path.join(_folder(comment), name)
    if os.path.isfile(path):
        os.remove(path)

    comment.image = names
    comment.save(update_fields=['image'])


# ============================================================
# ВНУТРЕННИЕ
# ============================================================
def _folder(comment):
    """
    PublicationStorage/PubItem_<pub_id>/Comments/ItemOfComment_<comment_id>/
    """
    root = str(settings.PUBLICATION_STORAGE_ROOT)
    return os.path.join(
        root,
        f'PubItem_{comment.publication_id}',
        'Comments',
        f'ItemOfComment_{comment.pk}',
    )

def _append_images(comment, files):
    folder = _folder(comment)
    os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
    names = list(comment.image_list)
    if len(names) + len(files) > MAX_IMAGES:
        raise ValidationError(f'Максимум {MAX_IMAGES} изображений на комментарий')
    max_num = 0
    if os.path.isdir(folder):
        for f in os.listdir(folder):
            if not f.startswith('image_item_'):
                continue
            stem, _ = os.path.splitext(f)
            parts = stem.split('_')
            if len(parts) >= 4 and parts[-1].isdigit():
                max_num = max(max_num, int(parts[-1]))
    number = max_num + 1
    for file in files:
        if file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
            raise ValidationError(
                f'Файл {file.name} больше {MAX_FILE_SIZE // (1024 * 1024)} МБ'
            )
        ext = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(file.name))[1].lower()
        if ext not in ALLOWED:
            raise ValidationError(f'Недопустимый формат файла: {file.name}')
        fname = f'image_item_{comment.pk}_{number}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}{ext}'
        number += 1
        with open(os.path.join(folder, fname), 'wb+') as dest:
            for chunk in file.chunks():
                dest.write(chunk)
        names.append(fname)
    comment.image = names
    comment.save(update_fields=['image'])

### FILE: comments\models.py

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import models
from core.models import Publication

class Comments(models.Model):
    publication = models.ForeignKey(Publication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='comments', verbose_name='Объявление')
    author = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='comments', verbose_name='Автор')
    text = models.TextField('Текст комментария')
    image = models.JSONField('Имена изображений', default=list, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Создано', auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Комментарий'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Комментарии'
        ordering = ['created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Комментарий от {self.author.username}'

    @property
    def image_list(self):
        v = self.image
        if isinstance(v, list):
            return [str(x) for x in v if x]
        if isinstance(v, str) and v:
            return [v]
        return []

    @property
    def image_urls(self):
        from urllib.parse import quote
        base = getattr(settings, 'PUBLICATION_STORAGE_URL', '/publication-files/').rstrip('/')
        prefix = (
            f'{base}/PubItem_{self.publication_id}'
            f'/Comments/ItemOfComment_{self.pk}' )
        return [f'{prefix}/{quote(n)}' for n in self.image_list]

================================================================================
### FILE: comments\views.py
================================================================================
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from core.models import Publication
from .forms import CommentForm
from .models import Comments
from . import comment_service

@login_required
@require_POST
def comment_create_view(request, slug):
    publication = get_object_or_404(Publication, slug=slug, status='active')
    form = CommentForm(request.POST)

    if form.is_valid():
        files = request.FILES.getlist('images_upload')
        comment_service.create_comment(form, request.user, publication, files)
        messages.success(request, 'Комментарий добавлен')
    else:
        messages.error(request, 'Комментарий не может быть пустым')
    return redirect(publication.get_absolute_url())

@login_required
@require_POST
def comment_update_view(request, pk):
    comment = get_object_or_404(Comments, pk=pk)
    text = (request.POST.get('text') or '').strip()
    if not text:
        messages.error(request, 'Текст не может быть пустым')
        return redirect(comment.publication.get_absolute_url())

    files = request.FILES.getlist('images_upload')
    comment_service.update_comment(comment, request.user, text, files)
    messages.success(request, 'Комментарий обновлён')
    return redirect(comment.publication.get_absolute_url())

@login_required
@require_POST
def comment_delete_view(request, pk):
    comment = get_object_or_404(Comments, pk=pk)
    publication_url = comment.publication.get_absolute_url()
    comment_service.delete_comment(comment, request.user)
    messages.success(request, 'Комментарий удалён')
    return redirect(publication_url)

@login_required
@require_POST
def comment_image_remove_view(request, pk, index):
    comment = get_object_or_404(Comments, pk=pk)
    comment_service.delete_comment_image(comment, index, request.user)
    messages.success(request, 'Фото удалено')
    return redirect(comment.publication.get_absolute_url())

from django.core.cache import cache
from .models import Category

def categories(request):
    cats = cache.get('menu_categories')
    if cats is None:
        cats = list(Category.objects.all())
        cache.set('menu_categories', cats, 60 * 10)
    return {'menu_categories': cats}

from django import forms
from .models import Publication


class PublicationForm(forms.ModelForm):
    tags_input = forms.CharField(
        label='Теги',
        required=False,
        widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={
            'class': 'form-control',
            'placeholder': 'Через запятую: дизайн, логотип, товар',
        }),
    )

    MAX_FILES = 10
    MAX_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024
    ALLOWED_EXT = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp'}

    class Meta:
        model = Publication
        fields = (
            'publication_type',
            'title',
            'description',
            'category',
            'price',
            'price_type',
            'deadline',
            'tags_input',
        )
        widgets = {
            'publication_type': forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'title': forms.TextInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'description': forms.Textarea(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'rows': 5}),
            'category': forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'price': forms.NumberInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'step': '0.01'}),
            'price_type': forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'form-control'}),
            'deadline': forms.DateInput(attrs={'class': 'form-control', 'type': 'date'}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
            tags = self.instance.tags.values_list('name', flat=True)
            self.fields['tags_input'].initial = ', '.join(tags)

    def clean(self):
        cleaned = super().clean()
        raw = cleaned.get('tags_input', '') or ''
        names = [n.strip() for n in raw.split(',') if n.strip()]
        if len(names) > 30:
            raise forms.ValidationError('Слишком много тегов (макс. 30).')
        for n in names:
            if len(n) > 50:
                raise forms.ValidationError(f'Тег «{n}» длиннее 50 символов.')
        cleaned['tags_input'] = ', '.join(names)

        files = self.files.getlist('images_upload') if self.files else []
        if len(files) > self.MAX_FILES:
            raise forms.ValidationError(
                f'Максимум {self.MAX_FILES} изображений за раз.'
            )
        for f in files:
            if f.size > self.MAX_SIZE:
                raise forms.ValidationError(
                    f'Файл «{f.name}» больше {self.MAX_SIZE // (1024 * 1024)} МБ.'
                )
            ext = ('.' + f.name.rsplit('.', 1)[-1].lower()) if '.' in f.name else ''
            if ext not in self.ALLOWED_EXT:
                raise forms.ValidationError(f'Недопустимый формат файла: {f.name}')

        return cleaned

### FILE: core\models.py

import os
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.storage import FileSystemStorage
from django.db import models, IntegrityError
from django.template.defaultfilters import slugify
from django.urls import reverse

class Tag(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField('название', max_length=50, unique=True)
    slug = models.SlugField('slug', max_length=60, unique=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['name']
        verbose_name = 'тег'
        verbose_name_plural = 'теги'

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.slug:
            self.slug = slugify(self.name)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

class Category(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField('название', max_length=80, unique=True)
    slug = models.SlugField('slug', max_length=90, unique=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['name']
        verbose_name = 'категория'
        verbose_name_plural = 'категории'

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.slug:
            self.slug = slugify(self.name)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

class Publication(models.Model):
    TYPE_CHOICES = (
        ('service', 'Услуга'),
        ('item', 'Товар'),
    )
    STATUS_CHOICES = (
        ('active', 'Активна'),
        ('in_progress', 'В работе'),
        ('closed', 'Закрыта'),
        ('archived', 'Архивирована'),
        ('frozen', 'Заблокирована'),
    )
    PRICE_TYPE_CHOICES = (
        ('fixed', 'Фиксированная'),
        ('hourly', 'Почасовая'),
        ('negotiable', 'Договорная'),
    )

    publication_type = models.CharField('Тип публикации', max_length=10, choices=TYPE_CHOICES, default='service')
    status = models.CharField('Статус', max_length=15, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='active')
    author = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='publications', verbose_name='Автор')
    category = models.ForeignKey(Category, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='publications', verbose_name='Категория')
    tags = models.ManyToManyField(Tag, blank=True, related_name='publications', verbose_name='Теги')
    title = models.CharField('Заголовок', max_length=200)
    slug = models.SlugField('Слаг', max_length=220, unique=True, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField('Описание')
    price = models.DecimalField('Цена', max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)
    price_type = models.CharField('Тип цены', max_length=10, choices=PRICE_TYPE_CHOICES, default='fixed')
    deadline = models.DateField('Срок выполнения', null=True, blank=True)

    image = models.JSONField('Имена изображений', default=list, blank=True)

    views = models.PositiveIntegerField('Просмотры', default=0)
    likes = models.PositiveIntegerField('Лайков', default=0)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Создано', auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField('Обновлено', auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'публикация'
        verbose_name_plural = 'публикации'
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['publication_type', 'status']),
            models.Index(fields=['-created_at']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

    @property
    def comments_count(self):
        return self.comments.count()

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.slug:
            base = slugify(self.title) or 'publication'
            slug = base
            i = 1
            while Publication.objects.filter(slug=slug).exclude(pk=self.pk).exists():
                slug = f'{base}-{i}'
                i += 1
            self.slug = slug
        try:
            super().save(*args, **kwargs)
        except IntegrityError:
            base = slugify(self.title) or 'publication'
            slug = base
            i = 1
            while Publication.objects.filter(slug=slug).exclude(pk=self.pk).exists():
                slug = f'{base}-{i}'
                i += 1
            self.slug = slug
            super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def get_absolute_url(self):
        return reverse('core:publication_detail', kwargs={'slug': self.slug})

    def get_price_display(self):
        if self.price is None:
            return 'Цена не указана'
        p = f'{self.price:.2f} ₽'
        if self.price_type == 'hourly':
            return f'{p} / час'
        if self.price_type == 'negotiable':
            return 'Договорная'
        return p

    @property
    def image_list(self):
        v = self.image
        if isinstance(v, list):
            return [str(x) for x in v if x]
        if isinstance(v, str) and v:
            return [v]
        return []

    @property
    def image_urls(self):
        base = getattr(settings, 'PUBLICATION_STORAGE_URL', '/publication-files/').rstrip('/')
        from urllib.parse import quote
        return [f'{base}/PubItem_{self.pk}/{quote(n)}' for n in self.image_list]

    @property
    def main_image_url(self):
        urls = self.image_urls
        return urls[0] if urls else None

    @property
    def likes_count(self):
        return self.favorited_by.count()

### FILE: core\publication_service.py
================================================================================
import os
import shutil
import uuid
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied, ValidationError
from django.db import transaction
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from django.template.defaultfilters import slugify

from core.models import Publication, Tag

ALLOWED = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp'}
MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_IMAGES = 10

# READ
def get_active_publication(slug):
    return get_object_or_404(Publication, slug=slug, status='active')

def get_publication_for_edit(slug, user):
    pub = get_object_or_404(Publication, slug=slug)
    if pub.author_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваша публикация')
    return pub

# CREATE
@transaction.atomic
def create_publication(form, user, files=None):
    pub = form.save(commit=False)
    pub.author = user
    pub.image = []
    pub.save()
    _save_tags(pub, form)
    if files:
        _append_images(pub, files)
    return pub

# UPDATE
@transaction.atomic
def update_publication(publication, form, files=None):
    obj = form.save(commit=False)
    obj.pk = publication.pk
    obj.save()
    _save_tags(obj, form)
    if files:
        _append_images(obj, files)
    return obj

# DELETE
@transaction.atomic
def delete_publication(publication):
    folder = _folder(publication)
    publication.delete()
    if os.path.isdir(folder):
        shutil.rmtree(folder, ignore_errors=True)

# DELETE
@transaction.atomic
def delete_publication_image(publication, index, user):
    if publication.author_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваша публикация')
    names = list(publication.image_list)
    if index < 0 or index >= len(names):
        return
    name = os.path.basename(names.pop(index))   # защита от path traversal
    path = os.path.join(_folder(publication), name)
    if os.path.isfile(path):
        os.remove(path)
    publication.image = names
    publication.save(update_fields=['image'])


def _folder(publication):
    return os.path.join(str(settings.PUBLICATION_STORAGE_ROOT), f'PubItem_{publication.pk}')

def _append_images(publication, files):
    folder = _folder(publication)
    os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
    names = list(publication.image_list)
    if len(names) + len(files) > MAX_IMAGES:
        raise ValidationError(f'Максимум {MAX_IMAGES} изображений на публикацию')
    max_num = 0
    if os.path.isdir(folder):
        for f in os.listdir(folder):
            if not f.startswith('image_item_'):
                continue
            stem, _ = os.path.splitext(f)
            parts = stem.split('_')
            if len(parts) >= 4 and parts[-1].isdigit():
                max_num = max(max_num, int(parts[-1]))
    number = max_num + 1

    for file in files:
        if file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
            raise ValidationError(
                f'Файл {file.name} больше {MAX_FILE_SIZE // (1024 * 1024)} МБ'
            )
        ext = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(file.name))[1].lower()
        if ext not in ALLOWED:
            raise ValidationError(f'Недопустимый формат файла: {file.name}')

        fname = f'image_item_{publication.pk}_{number}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}{ext}'
        number += 1

        with open(os.path.join(folder, fname), 'wb+') as dest:
            for chunk in file.chunks():
                dest.write(chunk)

        names.append(fname)

    publication.image = names
    publication.save(update_fields=['image'])

def _save_tags(publication, form):
    raw = form.cleaned_data.get('tags_input', '') or ''
    names = [n.strip() for n in raw.split(',') if n.strip()]
    tags = []
    for name in names:
        base_slug = slugify(name) or 'tag'
        slug = base_slug
        existing = Tag.objects.filter(slug=slug).first()
        if existing and existing.name != name:
            i = 1
            while Tag.objects.filter(slug=slug).exists():
                slug = f'{base_slug}-{i}'
                i += 1
        tag, _ = Tag.objects.get_or_create(name=name, defaults={'slug': slug})
        tags.append(tag)
    publication.tags.set(tags)

@transaction.atomic
def set_main_image(publication, index, user):
    if publication.author_id != user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваша публикация')
    names = list(publication.image_list)
    if index <= 0 or index >= len(names):
        return
    name = names.pop(index)
    names.insert(0, name)
    publication.image = names
    publication.save(update_fields=['image'])

================================================================================
### FILE: core\search_service.py
================================================================================
import logging

from django.contrib.postgres.search import TrigramSimilarity
from django.core.paginator import Paginator
from django.db import connection
from django.db.models import Case, Count, IntegerField, Max, Q, Value, When
from core.models import Publication
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 12
MIN_QUERY_LENGTH = 2
MAX_QUERY_LENGTH = 200
ALLOWED_SORTS = {'price', '-price', 'title', '-title', 'created_at', '-created_at'}
_TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE = None

def _has_trigram_extension():
    """
    Проверяет, установлено ли расширение pg_trgm.
    Результат кешируется на время жизни процесса.
    """
    global _TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE
    if _TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE is not None:
        return _TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE
    try:
        with connection.cursor() as cursor:
            cursor.execute("SELECT 1 FROM pg_extension WHERE extname = 'pg_trgm'")
            _TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE = cursor.fetchone() is not None
    except Exception:
        _TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE = False
    return _TRIGRAM_AVAILABLE


def _resolve_category_qs(qs, value):
    """Применяет фильтр по категории (id или slug)"""
    if not value:
        return qs
    if str(value).isdigit():
        return qs.filter(category_id=int(value))
    return qs.filter(category__slug=value)


def _resolve_tag_qs(qs, value):
    """Применяет фильтр по тегу (id или slug)"""
    if not value:
        return qs
    if str(value).isdigit():
        return qs.filter(tags__id=int(value))
    return qs.filter(tags__slug=value)


def search_publications(query, *, category=None, tag=None,price_min=None, price_max=None,sort=None, page=1):
    """
    Основная функция поиска публикаций
    Возвращает словарь с контекстом для шаблона
    """
    qs = (
        Publication.objects
        .filter(status='active')
        .select_related('author', 'category')
        .prefetch_related('tags')
        .annotate(likes_count_annotated=Count('favorited_by', distinct=True))
    )
    q = (query or '').strip()[:MAX_QUERY_LENGTH]
    has_query = len(q) >= MIN_QUERY_LENGTH
    if has_query:
        if _has_trigram_extension():
            qs = qs.annotate(
                _sim_title=TrigramSimilarity('title', q),
                _sim_desc=TrigramSimilarity('description', q),
            ).filter(
                Q(_sim_title__gt=0.1)
                | Q(_sim_desc__gt=0.1)
                | Q(title__icontains=q)
                | Q(tags__name__icontains=q)
            ).distinct()
        else:
            qs = qs.filter(
                Q(title__icontains=q)
                | Q(description__icontains=q)
                | Q(tags__name__icontains=q)
            ).distinct()
        qs = qs.annotate(
            _relevance=Max(
                Case(
                    When(title__icontains=q, then=Value(3)),
                    When(tags__name__icontains=q, then=Value(2)),
                    When(description__icontains=q, then=Value(1)),
                    default=Value(0),
                    output_field=IntegerField(),
                )
            )
        )

    qs = _resolve_category_qs(qs, category).distinct()
    qs = _resolve_tag_qs(qs, tag).distinct()

    if price_min:
        qs = qs.filter(price__gte=price_min)
    if price_max:
        qs = qs.filter(price__lte=price_max)

    if sort in ALLOWED_SORTS:
        qs = qs.order_by(sort)
    elif has_query:
        qs = qs.order_by('-_relevance', '-created_at')
    else:
        qs = qs.order_by('-created_at')

    paginator = Paginator(qs, DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)
    page_obj = paginator.get_page(page)

    return {
        'page_obj': page_obj,
        'total': paginator.count,
        'query': q,
        'has_query': has_query,
        'selected_category': category or '',
        'selected_tag': tag or '',
        'selected_sort': sort or '',
        'price_min': price_min or '',
        'price_max': price_max or '',
        'is_paginated': page_obj.has_other_pages(),
    }

### FILE: core\selectors.py
================================================================================
from django.core.paginator import Paginator
from django.db.models import Count, Q
from core.models import Publication
DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 12

def publication_queryset():
    """
    Базовый набор активных публикаций с author/category/tags
    и аннотацией likes_count_annotated.
    """
    return (
        Publication.objects
        .filter(status='active')
        .select_related('author', 'category')
        .prefetch_related('tags')
        .annotate(likes_count_annotated=Count('favorited_by', distinct=True))
    )


def apply_filters(qs, *, query=None, category=None, tag=None,price_min=None, price_max=None, sort=None):
    """
    Применяет пользовательские фильтры к queryset публикаций.
    - query       — строка поиска (по title/description/category/tags)
    - category    — ID (int/str) или slug категории
    - tag         — ID (int/str) или slug тега
    - price_min   — минимальная цена
    - price_max   — максимальная цена
    - sort        — одно из разрешённых значений
    """
    if query:
        qs = qs.filter(
            Q(title__icontains=query)
            | Q(description__icontains=query)
            | Q(category__name__icontains=query)
            | Q(tags__name__icontains=query)
            | Q(author__username__icontains=query)
        ).distinct()

    if category:
        if str(category).isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(category_id=int(category))
        else:
            qs = qs.filter(category__slug=category)

    if tag:
        if str(tag).isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(tags__id=int(tag))
        else:
            qs = qs.filter(tags__slug=tag)

    if price_min:
        qs = qs.filter(price__gte=price_min)
    if price_max:
        qs = qs.filter(price__lte=price_max)

    allowed_sorts = {'-created_at', 'created_at', 'price', '-price', 'title', '-title'}
    if sort in allowed_sorts:
        qs = qs.order_by(sort)
    else:
        qs = qs.order_by('-created_at')

    return qs


def attach_user_flags(qs, user):
    """
    Возвращает множества ID публикаций, которые пользователь лайкнул
    и которые лежат у него в корзине.
    Используется в шаблонах для отрисовки активных состояний без N+1.
    """
    from accounts.models import Favorite, Cart

    if not user.is_authenticated:
        return set(), set()

    pub_ids = qs.values_list('id', flat=True)
    liked = set(Favorite.objects.filter(user=user, publication_id__in=pub_ids)
                .values_list('publication_id', flat=True))
    cart = set(Cart.objects.filter(user=user, publication_id__in=pub_ids)
               .values_list('publication_id', flat=True))
    return liked, cart


def paginate(qs, page_number, page_size=DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE):
    """Оборачивает queryset в Paginator и возвращает текущую страницу."""
    paginator = Paginator(qs, page_size)
    return paginator.get_page(page_number), paginator


def build_category_sections():
    """
    Строит секции для главной ленты без N+1
    Один запрос, затем группировка в Python
    """
    from itertools import groupby
    from core.models import Category

    publications = list(
        Publication.objects
        .filter(status='active')
        .select_related('author', 'category')
        .prefetch_related('tags')
        .annotate(likes_count_annotated=Count('favorited_by', distinct=True))
        .order_by('category_id', '-created_at')
    )

    categories_by_id = {c.id: c for c in Category.objects.all()}

    sections = []
    for category_id, group in groupby(publications, key=lambda p: p.category_id):
        pubs = list(group)
        if category_id is None:
            sections.append({
                'category': None,
                'title': 'Разное',
                'publications': pubs,
            })
        else:
            sections.append({
                'category': categories_by_id.get(category_id),
                'publications': pubs,
            })
    return sections

### FILE: core\signals.py

from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db.models import F
from django.db.models.signals import post_save, post_delete
from django.dispatch import receiver
from accounts.models import Favorite
from .models import Category

@receiver([post_save, post_delete], sender=Category)
def clear_categories_cache(sender, **kwargs):
    cache.delete('menu_categories')

@receiver(post_save, sender=Favorite)
def like_created(sender, instance, created, **kwargs):
    if created:
        instance.publication.__class__.objects.filter(pk=instance.publication_id).update(likes=F('likes') + 1)

@receiver(post_delete, sender=Favorite)
def like_deleted(sender, instance, **kwargs):
    instance.publication.__class__.objects.filter(pk=instance.publication_id, likes__gt=0,).update(likes=F('likes') - 1)

from django.urls import path
from core import views

app_name = 'core'

urlpatterns = [
    path('', views.feed_view, name="feed"),
    path('publication_detail/<slug:slug>/', views.publication_detail_view, name='publication_detail'),
    path('publications/create/', views.publication_create_view, name='publication_create'),
    path('publications/<slug:slug>/edit/', views.publication_update_view, name='publication_update'),
    path('publications/<slug:slug>/remove/', views.publication_remove_view, name='publication_remove'),
    path('publications/<slug:slug>/image/<int:index>/remove/', views.publication_image_remove_view, name='publication_image_remove'),
    path('publications/<slug:slug>/image/<int:index>/set-main/', views.publication_image_set_main_view, name='publication_image_set_main'),
    path('publications/', views.publication_list_view, name='publication_list'),
    path('search/', views.search_view, name='search'),
]
