from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.db.models import F
from django.shortcuts import render, get_object_or_404, redirect
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST

from core import publication_service, search_service
from core.forms import PublicationForm
from core.models import Publication, Category, Tag
from core.selectors import (
    apply_filters,
    attach_user_flags,
    build_category_sections,
    paginate,
    publication_queryset,
)
from recommendations.logging_service import log_interaction
from recommendations.recommendation_service import get_recommendations

# ЛЕНТА
def feed_view(request):
    query = request.GET.get('q')
    category_id = request.GET.get('category')
    tag_id = request.GET.get('tag')
    price_min = request.GET.get('price_min')
    price_max = request.GET.get('price_max')
    sort = request.GET.get('sort', '-created_at')
    has_filters = any([query, category_id, tag_id, price_min, price_max, sort != '-created_at'])
    base_qs = publication_queryset()
    if has_filters:
        filtered = apply_filters(
            base_qs,
            query=query,
            category=category_id,
            tag=tag_id,
            price_min=price_min,
            price_max=price_max,
            sort=sort,
        )
        page_obj, paginator = paginate(filtered, request.GET.get('page'), page_size=12)
        liked_ids, cart_ids = attach_user_flags(filtered, request.user)
        sections = []
    else:
        page_obj = None
        paginator = None
        liked_ids, cart_ids = attach_user_flags(base_qs, request.user)
        sections = build_category_sections()

    recommendations = get_recommendations(request, limit=6)

    return render(request, 'core/feed.html', {
        'page_obj': page_obj,
        'recommendations': recommendations,
        'is_paginated': bool(page_obj and page_obj.has_other_pages()),
        'categories': Category.objects.all(),
        'tags': Tag.objects.all(),
        'query': query,
        'selected_category': category_id,
        'selected_tag': tag_id,
        'selected_sort': sort,
        'price_min': price_min,
        'price_max': price_max,
        'liked_ids': liked_ids,
        'cart_ids': cart_ids,
        'sections': sections,
        'has_filters': has_filters,
    })

# ДЕТАЛИ ПУБЛИКАЦИИ
def publication_detail_view(request, slug):
    """
    Страница публикации.
    Инкремент просмотров — атомарный UPDATE без refresh_from_db,
    чтобы не терять аннотацию likes_count_annotated
    """
    publication = get_object_or_404(publication_queryset(),slug=slug)
    Publication.objects.filter(pk=publication.pk).update(views=F('views') + 1)
    publication.views += 1

    try:
        log_interaction(request, 'view', publication=publication)
    except Exception:
        pass

    liked_ids, cart_ids = attach_user_flags(
        Publication.objects.filter(pk=publication.pk),
        request.user,
    )
    recommendations = get_recommendations(request, limit=4)

    return render(request, 'core/publication_detail.html', {
        'publication': publication,
        'liked_ids': liked_ids,
        'cart_ids': cart_ids,
        'recommendations': recommendations,
    })

# ПОИСК
def search_view(request):
    """
    Отдельная страница поиска с фильтрами.
    Релевантность и пагинация — в search_service
    """
    query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    result = search_service.search_publications(
        query=query,
        category=request.GET.get('category'),
        tag=request.GET.get('tag'),
        price_min=request.GET.get('price_min'),
        price_max=request.GET.get('price_max'),
        sort=request.GET.get('sort'),
        page=request.GET.get('page', 1),
    )
    try:
        log_interaction(request, 'search', search_query=query)
    except Exception:
        pass

    liked_ids, cart_ids = attach_user_flags(result['page_obj'].object_list, request.user)

    return render(request, 'core/search_results.html', {
        **result,
        'categories': Category.objects.all(),
        'tags': Tag.objects.all(),
        'liked_ids': liked_ids,
        'cart_ids': cart_ids,
    })


# ПУБЛИКАЦИИ ОДНОЙ КАТЕГОРИИ
def publication_list_view(request):
    """
    Публикации выбранной категории с сортировкой и пагинацией
    """
    category_val = request.GET.get('category')
    category = None

    qs = apply_filters(publication_queryset(), category=category_val)

    if category_val:
        if str(category_val).isdigit():
            category = Category.objects.filter(id=int(category_val)).first()
        else:
            category = Category.objects.filter(slug=category_val).first()

    sort = request.GET.get('sort', 'new')
    if sort == 'price_asc':
        qs = qs.order_by('price')
    elif sort == 'price_desc':
        qs = qs.order_by('-price')
    else:
        qs = qs.order_by('-created_at')

    page_obj, _ = paginate(qs, request.GET.get('page'), page_size=24)
    liked_ids, cart_ids = attach_user_flags(qs, request.user)

    return render(request, 'core/publication_list.html', {
        'category': category,
        'page_obj': page_obj,
        'sort': sort,
        'liked_ids': liked_ids,
        'cart_ids': cart_ids,
        'is_paginated': page_obj.has_other_pages(),
    })


# CRUD ПУБЛИКАЦИИ
@login_required
def publication_create_view(request):
    """Создание публикации"""
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = PublicationForm(request.POST, request.FILES)
        files = request.FILES.getlist('images_upload')
        if form.is_valid():
            publication_service.create_publication(form, request.user, files)
            messages.success(request, 'Публикация создана')
            return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=publications')
    else:
        form = PublicationForm()

    return render(request, 'core/publication_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'is_edit': False,
    })

@login_required
def publication_update_view(request, slug):
    """Редактирование своей публикации"""
    publication = publication_service.get_publication_for_edit(slug, request.user)

    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = PublicationForm(request.POST, request.FILES, instance=publication)
        files = request.FILES.getlist('images_upload')
        if form.is_valid():
            publication_service.update_publication(publication, form, files)
            messages.success(request, 'Изменения сохранены')
            return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=publications')
    else:
        form = PublicationForm(instance=publication)

    return render(request, 'core/publication_form.html', {
        'form': form,
        'publication': publication,
        'is_edit': True,
    })

@login_required
@require_POST
def publication_remove_view(request, slug):
    """Удаление своей публикации."""
    publication = publication_service.get_publication_for_edit(slug, request.user)
    publication_service.delete_publication(publication)
    messages.success(request, 'Публикация удалена')
    return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=publications')

@login_required
@require_POST
def publication_image_remove_view(request, slug, index):
    """Удаление отдельного изображения публикации"""
    publication = publication_service.get_publication_for_edit(slug, request.user)
    publication_service.delete_publication_image(publication, index, request.user)
    messages.success(request, 'Фото удалено')
    return redirect('core:publication_update', slug=slug)

@login_required
@require_POST
def publication_image_set_main_view(request, slug, index):
    """Назначение изображения основным"""
    publication = publication_service.get_publication_for_edit(slug, request.user)
    publication_service.set_main_image(publication, index, request.user)
    messages.success(request, 'Основное фото обновлено')
    return redirect('core:publication_update', slug=slug)

import os
from channels.auth import AuthMiddlewareStack
from channels.routing import ProtocolTypeRouter, URLRouter
from django.core.asgi import get_asgi_application
from main import settings

os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'main.settings')

django_asgi_app = get_asgi_application()

from chats.routing import websocket_urlpatterns  #noqa: E402

if settings.DEBUG:
    from django.contrib.staticfiles.handlers import ASGIStaticFilesHandler
    django_asgi_app = ASGIStaticFilesHandler(django_asgi_app)

application = ProtocolTypeRouter({
    'http': django_asgi_app,
    'websocket': AuthMiddlewareStack(
        URLRouter(websocket_urlpatterns)
    ),
})

from django.contrib import admin
from .models import Payment, Order, Transaction

class OrderInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = Order
    extra = 0
    fields = ('title', 'executor', 'amount', 'status')
    readonly_fields = ('title', 'executor', 'amount', 'status')
    can_delete = False

class TransactionInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = Transaction
    extra = 0
    fields = ('type', 'amount', 'status', 'external_id', 'created_at')
    readonly_fields = ('type', 'amount', 'status', 'external_id', 'created_at')
    can_delete = False

@admin.register(Payment)
class PaymentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('id', 'customer', 'amount', 'status', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('status', 'created_at')
    search_fields = ('customer__username', 'payment_id')
    inlines = [OrderInline, TransactionInline]

@admin.register(Order)
class OrderAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('id', 'title', 'customer', 'executor', 'amount', 'status', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('status', 'created_at')
    search_fields = ('title', 'customer__username', 'executor__username')
    inlines = [TransactionInline]

@admin.register(Transaction)
class TransactionAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ('id', 'type', 'amount', 'status', 'payment', 'order', 'created_at')
    list_filter = ('type', 'status')
    search_fields = ('external_id',)

import uuid
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models, transaction

class Payment(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICES = (('pending', 'Ожидает оплаты'), ('succeeded', 'Оплачен'), ('failed', 'Ошибка'), ('refunded', 'Возвращён'))
    customer = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='payments', verbose_name='Покупатель')
    amount = models.DecimalField('Сумма', max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    status = models.CharField('Статус', max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')
    payment_id = models.CharField('ID платежа YooKassa', max_length=100, blank=True)
    idempotence_key = models.UUIDField('Ключ идемпотентности', default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Создан', auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField('Обновлён', auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Платёж'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Платежи'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Платёж #{self.pk}: {self.amount} ₽'

class Order(models.Model):
    """Один товар от одного продавца - Часть платежа"""

    STATUS_CHOICES = (
        ('pending_payment', 'Ожидает оплаты'),
        ('escrow_held', 'Средства заморожены'),
        ('in_progress', 'В работе'),
        ('pending_review', 'На проверке'),
        ('completed', 'Завершён'),
        ('disputed', 'Спор'),
        ('cancelled', 'Отменён'),
        ('refunded', 'Возвращён'))

    payment = models.ForeignKey(Payment, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='orders', verbose_name='Платёж')
    customer = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='orders_as_customer', verbose_name='Заказчик')
    executor = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='orders_as_executor', verbose_name='Исполнитель')
    publication = models.ForeignKey('core.Publication', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='orders', verbose_name='Публикация')

    # снимок данных на момент покупки
    title = models.CharField('Название', max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField('Описание', blank=True)
    quantity = models.PositiveIntegerField('Количество', default=1)

    # финансы
    amount = models.DecimalField('Сумма', max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    platform_fee = models.DecimalField('Комиссия', max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)

    status = models.CharField('Статус', max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending_payment')
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Создан', auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField('Обновлён', auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Заказ'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Заказы'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f'Заказ #{self.pk}: {self.title}'

    # бизнес-логика

    def can_be_confirmed(self):
        return self.status in ('in_progress', 'pending_review', 'escrow_held')

    def can_be_cancelled(self):
        return self.status in ('pending_payment', 'escrow_held')

    @transaction.atomic
    def mark_as_escrow_held(self):
        """Деньги заморожены — вызывается из вебхука по Payment."""
        if self.status != 'pending_payment':
            return
        self.status = 'escrow_held'
        self.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        Transaction.objects.create(order=self, type='hold', status='succeeded', amount=self.amount)

    @transaction.atomic
    def confirm_completion(self):
        """Подтверждение - деньги уходят исполнителю"""
        if not self.can_be_confirmed():
            raise ValidationError('Нельзя подтвердить этот заказ')

        self.status = 'completed'
        self.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        Transaction.objects.create(order=self, type='capture', status='succeeded', amount=self.amount)
        Transaction.objects.create(order=self, type='payout', status='succeeded', amount=self.amount - self.platform_fee)

    @transaction.atomic
    def cancel_and_refund(self):
        """Отмена с возвратом"""
        if not self.can_be_cancelled():
            raise ValidationError('Нельзя отменить этот заказ')

        old_status = self.status
        self.status = 'refunded' if old_status == 'escrow_held' else 'cancelled'
        self.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        if old_status == 'escrow_held':
            Transaction.objects.create(order=self, type='refund', status='succeeded', amount=self.amount)

class Transaction(models.Model):
    """Финансовая операция"""

    TYPE_CHOICES = (('hold', 'Холдирование'), ('capture', 'Списание'), ('refund', 'Возврат'), ('payout', 'Выплата'))
    STATUS_CHOICES = (('pending', 'В ожидании'), ('succeeded', 'Успешно'), ('failed', 'Ошибка'))

    payment = models.ForeignKey(Payment, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, related_name='transactions')
    order = models.ForeignKey(Order, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, related_name='transactions')

    type = models.CharField('Тип', max_length=20, choices=TYPE_CHOICES)
    status = models.CharField('Статус', max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='pending')
    amount = models.DecimalField('Сумма', max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)

    external_id = models.CharField('ID в YooKassa', max_length=100, blank=True)
    idempotence_key = models.UUIDField('Ключ идемпотентности', default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Создана', auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField('Обновлена', auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Транзакция'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Транзакции'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

import ipaddress
from django.conf import settings

def get_client_ip(request):
    xff = request.META.get('HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR')
    if xff:
        return xff.split(',')[0].strip()
    return request.META.get('REMOTE_ADDR', '')

def is_yookassa_request(request):
    ip_string = get_client_ip(request)
    try:
        addr = ipaddress.ip_address(ip_string)
    except ValueError:
        return False

    for net in settings.YOOKASSA_TRUSTED_NETS:
        try:
            if addr in ipaddress.ip_network(net, strict=False):
                return True
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return False

import uuid
import logging
from django.conf import settings
from yookassa import Configuration, Payment as YooPayment, Refund
from .models import Transaction

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

Configuration.account_id = settings.YOOKASSA_SHOP_ID
Configuration.secret_key = settings.YOOKASSA_SECRET_KEY


def _assert_our_recipient(remote_object):
    """
    Проверяет, что платёж/возврат принадлежит нашему магазину.
    Защита от подделки вебхука чужим магазином.
    Возвращает True, если проверка пройдена (или поле отсутствует в ответе).
    """
    recipient = getattr(remote_object, 'recipient', None)
    if not recipient:
        return True
    account_id = getattr(recipient, 'account_id', None)
    if account_id is None:
        return True
    return account_id == settings.YOOKASSA_SHOP_ID


def create_payment_for_checkout(payment, return_url):
    """
    Создаёт в YooKassa единый платёж за корзину (Payment собирает несколько Order).
    Использует payment.idempotence_key — повторный вызов вернёт тот же платёж.

    ВАЖНО: при идемпотентном ответе, когда платёж уже прошёл стадию pending
    (waiting_for_capture / succeeded / canceled), в ответе YooKassa отсутствует
    поле confirmation — поэтому confirmation_url читается безопасно и может
    быть None. Вызывающий код решает, что делать по полю status.
    """
    try:
        yookassa_payment = YooPayment.create({
            'amount': {
                'value': f'{payment.amount:.2f}',
                'currency': 'RUB',
            },
            'capture': False,
            'confirmation': {
                'type': 'redirect',
                'return_url': return_url,
            },
            'description': f'Оплата {payment.orders.count()} товаров',
            'metadata': {
                'payment_id': payment.id,
            },
        }, str(payment.idempotence_key))
    except Exception as exc:
        payment.status = 'failed'
        payment.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
        raise RuntimeError(f'YooKassa error: {exc}') from exc

    payment.payment_id = yookassa_payment.id
    payment.save(update_fields=['payment_id', 'updated_at'])

    # Безопасное чтение confirmation: при идемпотентном ответе
    # confirmation может отсутствовать (None) — берём через getattr.
    confirmation = getattr(yookassa_payment, 'confirmation', None)
    confirmation_url = (
        getattr(confirmation, 'confirmation_url', None)
        if confirmation is not None
        else None
    )

    return {
        'payment_id': yookassa_payment.id,
        'confirmation_url': confirmation_url,
        'status': yookassa_payment.status,
    }


def create_escrow_payment(order, return_url):
    """
    Создаёт платёж под один Order (сценарий прямой оплаты без корзины)
    Идемпотентность — через Transaction.idempotence_key
    """
    tx = (
        Transaction.objects
        .filter(order=order, type='hold', status='pending')
        .order_by('-created_at')
        .first()
    )
    if tx is None:
        tx = Transaction.objects.create(
            order=order, type='hold', status='pending', amount=order.amount,
        )

    try:
        payment = YooPayment.create({
            'amount': {'value': f'{order.amount:.2f}', 'currency': 'RUB'},
            'capture': False,
            'confirmation': {'type': 'redirect', 'return_url': return_url},
            'description': f'Оплата заказа №{order.id}',
            'metadata': {'order_id': order.id},
        }, str(tx.idempotence_key))
    except Exception as exc:
        tx.status = 'failed'
        tx.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
        raise RuntimeError(f'YooKassa error: {exc}') from exc

    tx.external_id = payment.id
    tx.save(update_fields=['external_id', 'updated_at'])

    confirmation = getattr(payment, 'confirmation', None)
    confirmation_url = (
        getattr(confirmation, 'confirmation_url', None)
        if confirmation is not None
        else None
    )

    return {
        'payment_id': payment.id,
        'confirmation_url': confirmation_url,
        'status': payment.status,
    }

def capture_payment(payment_id, amount):
    """Списывает ранее холдированные средства"""
    return YooPayment.capture(
        payment_id,
        {'amount': {'value': f'{amount:.2f}', 'currency': 'RUB'}},
        str(uuid.uuid4()),
    )

def refund_payment(payment_id, amount):
    """Возвращает средства покупателю"""
    return Refund.create({
        'payment_id': payment_id,
        'amount': {'value': f'{amount:.2f}', 'currency': 'RUB'},
    })


def find_payment(payment_id):
    """Запрашивает платёж из YooKassa по его идентификатору"""
    return YooPayment.find_one(payment_id)

import json
import logging
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.core.exceptions import PermissionDenied
from django.db import transaction
from django.http import HttpResponse, HttpResponseBadRequest, HttpResponseForbidden
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST

from accounts.models import Cart
from .models import Payment, Order, Transaction
from .security import is_yookassa_request
from .services import (
    create_payment_for_checkout,
    find_payment,
    capture_payment,
    refund_payment,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ОФОРМЛЕНИЕ ЗАКАЗА

@login_required
@require_POST
def checkout_view(request):
    """
    Оформляет корзину в Payment + набор Order, затем редиректит на YooKassa.
    Вся работа с БД — в одной транзакции.
    """
    with transaction.atomic():
        cart_items = list(
            Cart.objects
            .select_for_update()
            .filter(user=request.user)
            .select_related('publication', 'publication__author')
        )
        cart_items = [i for i in cart_items if i.publication.author_id != request.user.id]

        if not cart_items:
            messages.error(request, 'Корзина пуста')
            return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=cart')

        total_amount = sum(
            (i.publication.price or Decimal('0')) * i.quantity
            for i in cart_items
        )
        if total_amount <= 0:
            messages.error(request, 'Некорректная сумма заказа')
            return redirect('/accounts/profile/?tab=cart')

        payment = Payment.objects.create(
            customer=request.user,
            amount=total_amount,
            status='pending',
        )

        for item in cart_items:
            pub = item.publication
            Order.objects.create(
                payment=payment,
                customer=request.user,
                executor=pub.author,
                publication=pub,
                title=pub.title,
                description=pub.description,
                quantity=item.quantity,
                amount=(pub.price or Decimal('0')) * item.quantity,
                status='pending_payment',
            )

        Cart.objects.filter(id__in=[i.id for i in cart_items]).delete()

    logger.info(
        'Checkout: payment #%s created for user %s, amount=%s',
        payment.id, request.user.id, total_amount,
    )

    return_url = request.build_absolute_uri(
        reverse('orders:payment_return', kwargs={'payment_id': payment.id})
    )
    try:
        result = create_payment_for_checkout(payment, return_url)
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        logger.exception('Failed to create YooKassa payment for Payment #%s', payment.id)
        messages.error(request, f'Не удалось создать платёж: {exc}')
        return redirect('orders:payment_detail', payment_id=payment.id)

    return _handle_payment_creation_result(request, payment, result)


# ОПЛАТА

@login_required
@require_POST
def pay_payment_view(request, payment_id):
    """
    Повторный платёж по существующему Payment (после отмены/неудачи/возврата
    пользователя назад). Идемпотентность обеспечивается Payment.idempotence_key:
    YooKassa вернёт тот же платёж, а мы по его статусу решим, куда редиректить.
    """
    payment = get_object_or_404(Payment, id=payment_id)
    if payment.customer_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш платёж')
    if payment.status != 'pending':
        raise PermissionDenied('Платёж уже обработан')
    if not payment.orders.exists():
        messages.error(request, 'Платёж не содержит заказов')
        return redirect('orders:payment_detail', payment_id=payment.id)

    return_url = request.build_absolute_uri(
        reverse('orders:payment_return', kwargs={'payment_id': payment.id})
    )
    try:
        result = create_payment_for_checkout(payment, return_url)
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        logger.exception('Failed to create YooKassa payment for Payment #%s', payment.id)
        messages.error(request, f'Не удалось создать платёж: {exc}')
        return redirect('orders:payment_detail', payment_id=payment.id)

    return _handle_payment_creation_result(request, payment, result)


@login_required
def payment_return_view(request, payment_id):
    """
    Возврат пользователя после оплаты.
    Пытается синхронизировать статус напрямую, но не блокирует страницу при ошибке.
    """
    payment = get_object_or_404(Payment, id=payment_id)
    if payment.customer_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш платёж')

    if payment.status == 'pending' and payment.payment_id:
        try:
            real = find_payment(payment.payment_id)
            _sync_payment_state(payment, real)
            payment.refresh_from_db()
        except Exception:
            logger.exception('Fallback sync failed for Payment #%s', payment.id)

    return render(request, 'orders/payment_return.html', {'payment': payment})


@login_required
def payment_detail_view(request, payment_id):
    """Экран платежа: сумма, статус, список привязанных заказов."""
    payment = get_object_or_404(
        Payment.objects.prefetch_related('orders__transactions'),
        id=payment_id,
    )
    if payment.customer_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш платёж')
    return render(request, 'orders/payment_detail.html', {'payment': payment})


# ДЕЙСТВИЯ ПО ЗАКАЗАМ

@login_required
@require_POST
def confirm_order_view(request, order_id):
    """Подтверждение выполнения заказа заказчиком; деньги уходят исполнителю."""
    order = get_object_or_404(Order, id=order_id)
    if order.customer_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Это не ваш заказ')
    if not order.can_be_confirmed():
        raise PermissionDenied('Нельзя подтвердить в текущем статусе')

    order.confirm_completion()
    logger.info('Order #%s confirmed by customer %s', order.id, request.user.id)
    messages.success(request, 'Заказ завершён')
    return redirect('orders:detail', order_id=order.id)


@login_required
@require_POST
def cancel_order_view(request, order_id):
    """Отмена заказа с возвратом средств, если они были заморожены."""
    order = get_object_or_404(Order, id=order_id)
    if order.customer_id != request.user.id and order.executor_id != request.user.id:
        raise PermissionDenied('Вы не участник этой сделки')
    if not order.can_be_cancelled():
        raise PermissionDenied('Нельзя отменить в текущем статусе')

    if order.payment and order.payment.payment_id and order.status == 'escrow_held':
        try:
            refund_payment(order.payment.payment_id, order.amount)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception('Refund failed for Order #%s', order.id)
            messages.error(request, f'Ошибка возврата: {exc}')
            return redirect('orders:detail', order_id=order.id)

    order.cancel_and_refund()
    logger.info('Order #%s cancelled and refunded', order.id)
    messages.success(request, 'Заказ отменён')
    return redirect('orders:detail', order_id=order.id)


# СПИСКИ

@login_required
def order_list_view(request):
    """Показывает заказы пользователя в двух ролях: заказчик и исполнитель."""
    as_customer = (
        Order.objects
        .filter(customer=request.user)
        .select_related('executor', 'publication', 'payment')
        .order_by('-created_at')
    )
    as_executor = (
        Order.objects
        .filter(executor=request.user)
        .select_related('customer', 'publication', 'payment')
        .order_by('-created_at')
    )
    return render(request, 'orders/order_list.html', {
        'as_customer': as_customer,
        'as_executor': as_executor,
    })


@login_required
def order_detail_view(request, order_id):
    """Детальная карточка заказа; доступна только участникам."""
    order = get_object_or_404(
        Order.objects
        .select_related('customer', 'executor', 'publication', 'payment')
        .prefetch_related('transactions'),
        id=order_id,
    )
    if request.user.id not in (order.customer_id, order.executor_id):
        raise PermissionDenied('Вы не участник этого заказа')
    return render(request, 'orders/order_detail.html', {'order': order})


# ЕДИНЫЙ ВЕБХУК YOOKASSA

@csrf_exempt
@require_POST
def yookassa_webhook_view(request):
    """
    Единая точка приёма вебхуков YooKassa.
    Проверяет IP отправителя, затем диспетчеризует по metadata.
    """
    if not is_yookassa_request(request):
        logger.warning('Webhook: forbidden source ip=%s', request.META.get('REMOTE_ADDR'))
        return HttpResponseForbidden('Forbidden')

    try:
        data = json.loads(request.body)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return HttpResponseBadRequest('Invalid JSON')

    obj = data.get('object', {})
    yookassa_payment_id = obj.get('id')
    metadata = obj.get('metadata', {})

    if 'method_id' in metadata:
        from accounts.pay_service import handle_binding_webhook
        return handle_binding_webhook(data)

    if 'payment_id' not in metadata or not yookassa_payment_id:
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    payment = Payment.objects.filter(id=metadata['payment_id']).first()
    if not payment:
        logger.warning('Webhook: Payment #%s not found', metadata['payment_id'])
        return HttpResponse(status=200)
    if payment.status == 'succeeded':
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    try:
        real = find_payment(yookassa_payment_id)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception('Webhook: cannot fetch YooKassa payment %s', yookassa_payment_id)
        return HttpResponseBadRequest('Payment not found')

    return _sync_payment_state(payment, real)


def _sync_payment_state(payment, real_payment):
    """
    Приводит Payment в актуальное состояние по данным YooKassa:
    - cancelled -> failed + отмена заказов
    - waiting_for_capture -> capture на полную сумму
    - succeeded -> финализация (холдирование + escrow-статус заказов)
    """
    recipient = getattr(real_payment, 'recipient', None)
    if recipient:
        account_id = getattr(recipient, 'account_id', None)
        if account_id and account_id != settings.YOOKASSA_SHOP_ID:
            logger.error('Webhook: recipient mismatch for Payment #%s', payment.id)
            return HttpResponseBadRequest('Recipient mismatch')

    try:
        real_amount = Decimal(str(real_payment.amount.value))
    except (AttributeError, InvalidOperation, ValueError):
        logger.error('Invalid YooKassa amount for Payment #%s', payment.id)
        return HttpResponseBadRequest('Invalid amount')

    if real_amount != payment.amount:
        logger.error(
            'Amount mismatch for Payment #%s: DB=%s, YooKassa=%s',
            payment.id, payment.amount, real_amount,
        )
        return HttpResponseBadRequest('Amount mismatch')

    status = getattr(real_payment, 'status', None)

    if status == 'canceled':
        with transaction.atomic():
            p = Payment.objects.select_for_update().get(id=payment.id)
            if p.status == 'pending':
                p.status = 'failed'
                p.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
                p.orders.update(status='cancelled')
        logger.info('Payment #%s canceled', payment.id)
        return HttpResponse(status=200)

    if status == 'waiting_for_capture':
        try:
            capture_payment(real_payment.id, payment.amount)
            logger.info(
                'Captured YooKassa payment %s for %s',
                real_payment.id, payment.amount,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception('Capture failed for Payment #%s', payment.id)
            return HttpResponseBadRequest(f'Capture failed: {exc}')

        try:
            real_payment = find_payment(real_payment.id)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception('Re-fetch after capture failed for Payment #%s', payment.id)
            return HttpResponse(status=200)

        status = getattr(real_payment, 'status', None)

    if status == 'succeeded':
        _finalize_payment_success(payment, real_payment.id)

    return HttpResponse(status=200)


def _finalize_payment_success(payment, yookassa_payment_id):
    """
    Финализация успешного платежа: статус succeeded, Transaction hold,
    заказы в escrow_held, очистка корзины. Идемпотентна.
    """
    with transaction.atomic():
        p = Payment.objects.select_for_update().get(id=payment.id)
        if p.status == 'succeeded':
            return

        p.status = 'succeeded'
        if not p.payment_id:
            p.payment_id = yookassa_payment_id
        p.save(update_fields=['status', 'payment_id', 'updated_at'])

        Transaction.objects.create(
            payment=p,
            type='hold',
            status='succeeded',
            amount=p.amount,
            external_id=yookassa_payment_id,
        )

        for order in p.orders.all():
            order.mark_as_escrow_held()

        pub_ids = list(p.orders.values_list('publication_id', flat=True))
        Cart.objects.filter(user=p.customer, publication_id__in=pub_ids).delete()

    logger.info('Payment #%s finalized, orders moved to escrow_held', payment.id)


# ОБРАБОТКА РЕЗУЛЬТАТА СОЗДАНИЯ ПЛАТЕЖА

def _handle_payment_creation_result(request, payment, result):
    """
    Единая обработка ответа create_payment_for_checkout.
    Возможные сценарии:
    - есть confirmation_url  -> редирект на YooKassa (обычный путь);
    - canceled               -> помечаем failed, отменяем заказы;
    - waiting_for_capture    -> синхронизируем (capture) и показываем «в обработке»;
    - succeeded              -> синхронизируем и показываем «уже оплачен».
    """
    confirmation_url = result.get('confirmation_url')
    if confirmation_url:
        return redirect(confirmation_url)

    remote_status = result.get('status')
    remote_id = result.get('payment_id')

    if remote_status == 'canceled':
        with transaction.atomic():
            payment.status = 'failed'
            payment.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
            payment.orders.update(status='cancelled')
        messages.error(request, 'Платёж был отменён. Оформите заказ заново.')
        return redirect('orders:payment_detail', payment_id=payment.id)

    # succeeded / waiting_for_capture — синхронизируем состояние с YooKassa
    try:
        real = find_payment(remote_id)
        _sync_payment_state(payment, real)
        payment.refresh_from_db()
    except Exception:
        logger.exception(
            'Не удалось синхронизировать платёж #%s после идемпотентного ответа',
            payment.id,
        )

    if payment.status == 'succeeded':
        messages.success(request, 'Платёж уже оплачен')
    elif payment.status == 'failed':
        messages.error(request, 'Платёж не прошёл. Оформите заказ заново.')
    else:
        messages.info(request, 'Платёж находится в обработке. Проверьте статус позже.')

    return redirect('orders:payment_detail', payment_id=payment.id)

from .models import UserInteraction

def log_interaction(request, action_type, *, publication=None, search_query=None):
    """
    Записывает одно действие пользователя в историю.

    :param request: HttpRequest (нужен для доступа к user и session)
    :param action_type: 'view' | 'like' | 'cart' | 'search'
    :param publication: объект Publication или None
    :param search_query: строка поиска (только для action_type='search')
    """
    if action_type not in dict(UserInteraction.ACTION_CHOICES):
        return None
    if action_type == 'search':
        if not search_query or len(search_query.strip()) < 2:
            return None
        search_query = search_query.strip()
    user = request.user if request.user.is_authenticated else None
    session_key = None
    if not user:
        if not request.session.session_key:
            request.session.create()
        session_key = request.session.session_key
    return UserInteraction.objects.create(
        user=user,
        session_key=session_key,
        publication=publication,
        action_type=action_type,
        search_query=search_query,
    )

================================================================================
### FILE: recommendations\models.py
================================================================================
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings

class UserInteraction(models.Model):
    ACTION_CHOICES = (
        ('view', 'Просмотр'),
        ('like', 'Лайк'),
        ('cart', 'В корзину'),
        ('search', 'Поиск'),
    )

    user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Пользователь')
    session_key = models.CharField('Ключ сессии', max_length=40, null=True, blank=True)
    publication = models.ForeignKey('core.Publication', on_delete=models.CASCADE, null=True, blank=True, verbose_name='Публикация')
    action_type = models.CharField('Действие', max_length=10, choices=ACTION_CHOICES)
    search_query = models.CharField('Поисковый запрос', max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField('Дата действия', auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-created_at']
        verbose_name = 'Взаимодействие'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Взаимодействия'
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['user', 'action_type']),
            models.Index(fields=['session_key', 'action_type']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        subject = self.user.username if self.user else f"Аноним ({self.session_key[:8]}...)"
        target = self.publication.title if self.publication else self.search_query
        return f"{subject} -> {self.get_action_type_display()} -> {target}"

================================================================================
### FILE: recommendations\recommendation_service.py
================================================================================
import hashlib
import logging
import re

from django.core.cache import cache
from django.db.models import Count, Max
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import linear_kernel

from core.models import Publication
from .models import UserInteraction

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
MATRIX_CACHE_TTL = 60 * 15          # 15 минут
RECOMMENDATION_CACHE_TTL = 60 * 5   # 5 минут
MAX_INTERACTIONS = 10


def _matrix_cache_key():
    """
    Строит ключ инвалидации матрицы: меняется, когда меняется набор
    активных публикаций (по количеству / максимальному id / свежести).
    """
    stats = Publication.objects.filter(status='active').aggregate(
        n=Count('id'),
        max_pk=Max('id'),
        last=Max('updated_at'),
    )
    raw = f"{stats['n']}|{stats['max_pk']}|{stats['last']}"
    return 'reco:matrix:' + hashlib.md5(raw.encode()).hexdigest()


def _get_vectorizer_and_matrix():
    """
    Возвращает (publications, tfidf_matrix) для активных публикаций
    Матрица кешируется; пересчитывается только при изменении публикаций
    """
    key = _matrix_cache_key()
    cached = cache.get(key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached

    pubs = list(
        Publication.objects
        .filter(status='active')
        .prefetch_related('tags', 'category')
    )
    if not pubs:
        return None

    corpus = []
    for pub in pubs:
        tags = " ".join(t.name for t in pub.tags.all())
        category = pub.category.name if pub.category else ""
        text = f"{pub.title} {pub.title} {category} {category} {tags} {pub.description}"
        corpus.append(re.sub(r'[^\w\s]', '', text.lower()))

    vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer()
    matrix = vectorizer.fit_transform(corpus)

    cache.set(key, (pubs, matrix), MATRIX_CACHE_TTL)
    return pubs, matrix

def _user_cache_key(user, session_key, limit):
    """Ключ кеша рекомендаций для конкретного пользователя или сессии."""
    identity = f"u{user.id}" if user else f"s{session_key}"
    return f'reco:{identity}:{limit}'


def get_recommendations(request, limit=6):
    """
    Возвращает список рекомендованных публикаций.
    - Аноним без сессии  -> популярные.
    - Нет истории        -> популярные.
    - Иначе              -> TF-IDF-похожесть по лайкам/просмотрам/корзине.
    Кешируется на 5 минут.
    """
    user = request.user if request.user.is_authenticated else None
    session_key = request.session.session_key if not user else None

    if not user and not session_key:
        return _get_popular_publications(limit)

    cache_key = _user_cache_key(user, session_key, limit)
    cached = cache.get(cache_key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached

    interaction_qs = (
        UserInteraction.objects
        .filter(
            user=user,
            session_key=session_key,
            action_type__in=['like', 'view', 'cart'],
            publication__isnull=False,
        )
        .values_list('publication_id', flat=True)
        .distinct()[:MAX_INTERACTIONS]
    )
    interaction_ids = list(interaction_qs)

    if not interaction_ids:
        result = _get_popular_publications(limit)
        cache.set(cache_key, result, RECOMMENDATION_CACHE_TTL)
        return result

    built = _get_vectorizer_and_matrix()
    if not built:
        return _get_popular_publications(limit)

    pubs, matrix = built
    idx_by_id = {pub.id: i for i, pub in enumerate(pubs)}
    target_idx = [idx_by_id[pid] for pid in interaction_ids if pid in idx_by_id]
    if not target_idx:
        result = _get_popular_publications(limit)
        cache.set(cache_key, result, RECOMMENDATION_CACHE_TTL)
        return result
    cosine_similarities = linear_kernel(matrix[target_idx], matrix).mean(axis=0)
    related_indices = cosine_similarities.argsort()[::-1]
    recommendations = []
    for i in related_indices:
        pub = pubs[i]
        if pub.id not in interaction_ids:
            recommendations.append(pub)
            if len(recommendations) >= limit:
                break
    cache.set(cache_key, recommendations, RECOMMENDATION_CACHE_TTL)
    return recommendations

def _get_popular_publications(limit):
    """Топ публикаций по лайкам — фолбэк для холодного старта"""
    return list(
        Publication.objects.filter(status='active')
        .annotate(total_likes=Count('favorited_by'))
        .order_by('-total_likes', '-created_at')[:limit]
    )

### FILE: static\js\account.js

(function () {
    'use strict';

    const menuItems = document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item');
    const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.content-panel');

    function activateTab(tabName) {
        menuItems.forEach(b => b.classList.remove('menu-active'));
        panels.forEach(p => p.classList.remove('content-active'));

        const btn = document.querySelector('.menu-item[data-tab="' + tabName + '"]');
        const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + tabName);

        if (btn) btn.classList.add('menu-active');
        if (panel) panel.classList.add('content-active');
    }

    menuItems.forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => activateTab(btn.dataset.tab));
    });

    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const initialTab = params.get('tab');
    if (initialTab) activateTab(initialTab);

    // 2. КОРЗИНА
    const cartForm = document.getElementById('cart-form');
    if (!cartForm) return;

    const checkboxes = Array.from(cartForm.querySelectorAll('.cart-checkbox'));
    const selectAll = document.getElementById('select-all');
    const totalEl = document.getElementById('cart-total-amount');
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById('cart-checkout-btn');

    function recalcTotal() {
        let total = 0;
        let selectedCount = 0;

        checkboxes.forEach(cb => {
            if (cb.checked) {
                total += parseFloat(cb.dataset.price) || 0;
                selectedCount++;
            }
        });

        if (totalEl) {
            totalEl.textContent = Math.round(total).toLocaleString('ru-RU') + ' ₽';
        }

        if (submitBtn) {
            submitBtn.disabled = selectedCount === 0;
            submitBtn.classList.toggle('is-disabled', selectedCount === 0);
        }
    }

    checkboxes.forEach(cb => {
        cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
            if (selectAll) {
                selectAll.checked = checkboxes.every(c => c.checked);
            }
            recalcTotal();
        });
    });

    if (selectAll) {
        selectAll.addEventListener('change', () => {
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = selectAll.checked);
            recalcTotal();
        });
    }
    // УДАЛЕНИЕ ТОВАРА ИЗ КОРЗИНЫ
    const removeForm = document.getElementById('cart-remove-form');

    document.querySelectorAll('.cart-remove-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();

            const url = btn.dataset.removeUrl;
            if (!url || !removeForm) return;
            if (!confirm('Убрать товар из корзины?')) return;

            removeForm.action = url;
            removeForm.submit();
        });
    });

    recalcTotal();
})();

================================================================================
### FILE: static\js\chat_detail.js
================================================================================
(function () {
    'use strict';

    const root = document.querySelector('.chat-detail');
    if (!root) return;

    const chatId = root.dataset.chatId;
    const currentUserId = parseInt(root.dataset.userId, 10);
    const messagesDiv = document.getElementById('messages');
    const input = document.getElementById('message-input');
    const form = document.getElementById('send-form');

    if (!messagesDiv || !input || !form || !chatId) {
        console.warn('[chat] не хватает элементов для инициализации');
        return;
    }

    // WEBSOCKET
    const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';
    const socket = new WebSocket(`${protocol}://${window.location.host}/ws/chat/${chatId}/`);


    function scrollToBottom() {
        messagesDiv.scrollTop = messagesDiv.scrollHeight;
    }

    function escapeHtml(str) {
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.textContent = str;
        return div.innerHTML;
    }

    function formatTime(isoString) {
        const date = new Date(isoString);
        return date.toLocaleString('ru-RU', {
            day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit',
            hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
        });
    }

    function appendMessage(data) {
        const empty = messagesDiv.querySelector('.messages-empty');
        if (empty) empty.remove();

        const isOwn = parseInt(data.author_id, 10) === currentUserId;
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'message ' + (isOwn ? 'message-own' : 'message-other');
        el.dataset.messageId = data.message_id;

        const authorHtml = isOwn
            ? ''
            : `<span class="message-author">${escapeHtml(data.author)}</span>`;

        el.innerHTML = `
            <div class="message-bubble">
                ${authorHtml}
                <span class="message-text">${escapeHtml(data.text)}</span>
                <span class="message-time">${formatTime(data.created_at)}</span>
            </div>
        `;

        messagesDiv.appendChild(el);
        scrollToBottom();
    }

    // СОБЫТИЯ WEBSOCKET
    socket.onopen = () => {
        console.log('[chat] WebSocket открыт');
    };

    socket.onmessage = (e) => {
        try {
            appendMessage(JSON.parse(e.data));
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('[chat] ошибка парсинга сообщения:', err);
        }
    };

    socket.onerror = (e) => {
        console.error('[chat] WebSocket ошибка:', e);
    };

    socket.onclose = (e) => {
        if (e.wasClean) {
            console.log('[chat] WebSocket закрыт');
        } else {
            console.warn('[chat] WebSocket оборван. Обновите страницу.');
        }
    };

    function sendMessage() {
        const text = input.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        if (socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
            alert('Соединение потеряно. Обновите страницу.');
            return;
        }

        socket.send(JSON.stringify({ text: text }));
        input.value = '';
        input.style.height = 'auto';
    }

    form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
    });

    input.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
            e.preventDefault();
            sendMessage();
        }
    });

    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
        input.style.height = 'auto';
        input.style.height = Math.min(input.scrollHeight, 120) + 'px';
    });

    scrollToBottom();
    input.focus();
})();

### FILE: static\js\comments.js
================================================================================
(function () {
    'use strict';

    var MAX_FILES = 5;

    function renderPreview(box, files) {
        box.innerHTML = '';
        files.forEach(function (file, index) {
            if (!file.type || file.type.indexOf('image/') !== 0) return;
            var wrap = document.createElement('div');
            wrap.className = 'comment-preview-item';
            wrap.title = file.name;
            var img = document.createElement('img');
            img.alt = file.name;
            var btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.className = 'comment-preview-remove';
            btn.setAttribute('data-index', String(index));
            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Убрать фото');
            btn.textContent = '×';
            var reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function (e) { img.src = e.target.result; };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            wrap.appendChild(img);
            wrap.appendChild(btn);
            box.appendChild(wrap);
        });

        if (files.length >= MAX_FILES) {
            var warn = document.createElement('div');
            warn.className = 'comment-preview-warning';
            warn.textContent = 'Достигнут лимит: ' + MAX_FILES + ' фото';
            box.appendChild(warn);
        }
    }

    function bindInput(input) {
        var targetId = input.getAttribute('data-preview-target');
        if (!targetId) return;
        var box = document.getElementById(targetId);
        if (!box) return;

        var selectedFiles = [];

        function syncInput() {
            try {
                var dt = new DataTransfer();
                selectedFiles.forEach(function (f) { dt.items.add(f); });
                input.files = dt.files;
            } catch (e) {

            }
        }

        function isDuplicate(file) {
            return selectedFiles.some(function (x) {
                return x.name === file.name && x.size === file.size;
            });
        }

        input.addEventListener('change', function () {
            var picked = Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files || []);

            picked.forEach(function (file) {
                if (selectedFiles.length >= MAX_FILES) return;
                if (isDuplicate(file)) return;
                selectedFiles.push(file);
            });

            syncInput();
            renderPreview(box, selectedFiles);
        });

        box.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            var btn = e.target.closest('.comment-preview-remove');
            if (!btn) return;

            var idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-index'), 10);
            if (isNaN(idx) || idx < 0 || idx >= selectedFiles.length) return;

            selectedFiles.splice(idx, 1);
            syncInput();
            renderPreview(box, selectedFiles);
        });
    }

    function init() {
        var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.comment-file-input');
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(inputs, bindInput);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        init();
    }
})();

### FILE: static\js\feed.js

(function () {
    'use strict';
    const SCROLL_STEP = 480;
    document.querySelectorAll('.carousel').forEach(function (carousel) {
        const track = carousel.querySelector('.carousel-track');
        if (!track) return;
        const prevBtn = document.querySelector(
            '.carousel-prev[data-target="' + carousel.id + '"]'
        );
        const nextBtn = document.querySelector(
            '.carousel-next[data-target="' + carousel.id + '"]'
        );
        function updateControls() {
            const scrollLeft = track.scrollLeft;
            const maxScroll = track.scrollWidth - track.clientWidth;

            if (prevBtn) {
                prevBtn.disabled = scrollLeft <= 1;
            }
            if (nextBtn) {
                nextBtn.disabled = scrollLeft >= maxScroll - 1;
            }
            carousel.classList.toggle('has-left', scrollLeft > 1);
            carousel.classList.toggle('has-right', scrollLeft < maxScroll - 1);
        }
        if (prevBtn) {
            prevBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                track.scrollBy({ left: -SCROLL_STEP, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });
        }
        if (nextBtn) {
            nextBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                track.scrollBy({ left: SCROLL_STEP, behavior: 'smooth' });
            });
        }
        track.addEventListener('scroll', updateControls);
        window.addEventListener('resize', updateControls);
        updateControls();
    });
})();

(function () {
    'use strict';
    function getCookie(name) {
        const m = document.cookie.match('(^|;)\\s*' + name + '\\s*=\\s*([^;]+)');
        return m ? m.pop() : '';
    }

    const CSRF = getCookie('csrftoken');

    document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
        const form = e.target;
        if (!form.classList.contains('ajax-action')) return;

        e.preventDefault();

        const button = form.querySelector('button');
        if (!button || button.disabled) return;
        button.disabled = true;

        fetch(form.action, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                'X-CSRFToken': CSRF,
            },
            credentials: 'same-origin',
        })
        .then(function (r) {
            if (r.redirected && r.url.indexOf('/login') !== -1) {
                window.location.href = r.url;
                throw new Error('redirected to login');
            }
            if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
            return r.json();
        })
        .then(function (data) {
            //ЛАЙК
            if (typeof data.liked !== 'undefined') {
                button.classList.toggle('is-liked', data.liked);

                if (typeof data.likes_count !== 'undefined') {
                    const m = form.action.match(/\/like\/(\d+)\//);
                    if (m) {
                        document
                            .querySelectorAll('form[action*="/like/' + m[1] + '/"] .like-count')
                            .forEach(function (el) {
                                el.textContent = data.likes_count;
                            });
                    }
                }
            }
            //КОРЗИНА
            if (typeof data.in_cart !== 'undefined') {
                button.classList.toggle('is-active', data.in_cart);
                const label = button.querySelector('.cart-label');
                if (label) {
                    label.textContent = data.in_cart ? 'В корзине' : 'В корзину';
                }
            }
        })
        .catch(function (err) {
            if (err && err.message !== 'redirected to login') {
                console.error('Action failed:', err);
            }
        })
        .finally(function () {
            button.disabled = false;
        });
    });
})();

================================================================================
### FILE: static\js\publication_form.js
================================================================================
(function () {
    'use strict';

    var MAX_FILES = 10;

    function renderPreview(box, files) {
        box.innerHTML = '';
        files.forEach(function (file, index) {
            if (!file.type || file.type.indexOf('image/') !== 0) return;
            var wrap = document.createElement('div');
            wrap.className = 'pub-preview-item';
            wrap.title = file.name;
            var img = document.createElement('img');
            img.alt = file.name;
            var btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.className = 'pub-preview-remove';
            btn.setAttribute('data-index', String(index));
            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Убрать фото');
            btn.textContent = '×';
            var reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function (e) { img.src = e.target.result; };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            wrap.appendChild(img);
            wrap.appendChild(btn);
            box.appendChild(wrap);
        });

        if (files.length >= MAX_FILES) {
            var warn = document.createElement('div');
            warn.className = 'pub-preview-warning';
            warn.textContent = 'Достигнут лимит: ' + MAX_FILES + ' фото';
            box.appendChild(warn);
        }
    }

    function bindInput(input) {
        var targetId = input.getAttribute('data-preview-target');
        if (!targetId) return;
        var box = document.getElementById(targetId);
        if (!box) return;
        var selectedFiles = [];

        function syncInput() {
            try {
                var dt = new DataTransfer();
                selectedFiles.forEach(function (f) { dt.items.add(f); });
                input.files = dt.files;
            } catch (e) {

            }
        }

        function isDuplicate(file) {
            return selectedFiles.some(function (x) {
                return x.name === file.name && x.size === file.size;
            });
        }

        input.addEventListener('change', function () {
            var picked = Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files || []);

            picked.forEach(function (file) {
                if (selectedFiles.length >= MAX_FILES) return;
                if (isDuplicate(file)) return;
                selectedFiles.push(file);
            });

            syncInput();
            renderPreview(box, selectedFiles);
        });

        box.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            var btn = e.target.closest('.pub-preview-remove');
            if (!btn) return;

            var idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-index'), 10);
            if (isNaN(idx) || idx < 0 || idx >= selectedFiles.length) return;

            selectedFiles.splice(idx, 1);
            syncInput();
            renderPreview(box, selectedFiles);
        });
    }

    function init() {
        var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.pub-images-input');
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(inputs, bindInput);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        init();
    }
})();

                </h1>

                <form method="post"
                      action="{% url 'accounts:payment_method_add' %}"
                      class="add-card-form">
                    {% csrf_token %}
                    <button type="submit" class="add-card-btn">
                        <span class="plus-icon">+</span>
                        Добавить карту
                    </button>
                </form>
            </div>

            {% if payment_methods %}
                <div class="payment-methods-list">
                    {% for method in payment_methods %}
                        <div class="payment-method-card {% if method.is_default %}is-default{% endif %}">

                            <form method="post"
                                  action="{% url 'accounts:payment_method_edit' method.id %}?tab=payments"
                                  class="pm-edit-form">
                                {% csrf_token %}
                                <input type="text"
                                       name="title"
                                       value="{{ method.title }}"
                                       placeholder="Название карты"
                                       maxlength="100"
                                       class="pm-input">
                                <button type="submit" class="pm-save-btn" title="Сохранить название">✓</button>
                            </form>

                            <div class="pm-info">
                                {% if method.is_pending %}
                                    <div class="pm-card pm-card-pending">
                                        Привязка карты…
                                    </div>
                                {% elif method.card_last4 %}
                                    <div class="pm-card">
                                        <span class="pm-card-type">{{ method.card_type|default:'Карта' }}</span>
                                        <span class="pm-card-mask">••••</span>
                                        <span class="pm-card-last4">{{ method.card_last4 }}</span>
                                    </div>
                                {% else %}
                                    <div class="pm-card pm-card-empty">
                                        {{ method.title|default:'Карта (данные недоступны)' }}
                                    </div>
                                {% endif %}
                                <div class="pm-date">
                                    Добавлена {{ method.created_at|date:"d.m.Y" }}
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            {% if method.is_default %}
                                <span class="pm-badge">Основная</span>
                            {% endif %}

                            <div class="pm-actions">
                                {% if not method.is_default and not method.is_pending %}

                                          action="{% url 'accounts:payment_method_default' method.id %}?tab=payments">
                                        {% csrf_token %}
                                        <button type="submit" class="pm-btn">Сделать основной</button>
                                    </form>
                                {% endif %}

                                <form method="post"
                                      action="{% url 'accounts:payment_method_remove' method.id %}?tab=payments"
                                      onsubmit="return confirm('Удалить карту •••• {{ method.card_last4|default:'?' }}?');">
                                    {% csrf_token %}
                                    <button type="submit" class="pm-btn pm-btn-danger">Удалить</button>
                                </form>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
            {% else %}
                <div class="content-empty">
                    <p>У вас нет сохранённых способов оплаты</p>

{% extends 'base.html' %}

{% block title %}{% if is_edit %}Редактирование{% else %}Новая публикация{% endif %} — Helium{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<section class="account-box">
    <h1>{% if is_edit %}Редактирование{% else %}Новая публикация{% endif %}</h1>

    <form method="post"
          enctype="multipart/form-data"
          class="content-form"
          id="publication-form">
        {% csrf_token %}

        {% if form.non_field_errors %}
            <div class="error">{{ form.non_field_errors.0 }}</div>
        {% endif %}

        {% for field in form %}
            <div class="form-group">
                <label for="{{ field.id_for_label }}">
                    {{ field.label }}
                    {% if field.field.required %}
                        <span class="required-mark">*</span>
                    {% endif %}
                </label>
                {{ field }}
                {% if field.help_text %}
                    <small class="field-help">{{ field.help_text }}</small>
                {% endif %}
                {% if field.errors %}
                    <div class="error">{{ field.errors.0 }}</div>
                {% endif %}
            </div>
        {% endfor %}

        <div class="form-group">
            <label for="images_upload">
                {% if is_edit %}Добавить фото{% else %}Фотографии{% endif %}
            </label>
            <input type="file"
                   name="images_upload"
                   id="images_upload"
                   multiple
                   accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/gif,image/webp"
                   class="form-control pub-images-input"
                   data-preview-target="pub-images-preview">
            <small class="field-help">
                Можно выбрать сразу несколько файлов. Повторный выбор добавляет к уже выбранным.
                JPG, PNG, GIF, WEBP — до 5 МБ, макс. 10 шт.
            </small>

            <div class="pub-preview" id="pub-images-preview"></div>
        </div>

        {% if is_edit %}
            {% with urls=publication.image_urls %}
                {% if urls %}
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label>Загруженные фото ({{ urls|length }})</label>
                        <div class="existing-photos">
                            {% for url in urls %}
                                <div class="existing-photo {% if forloop.first %}is-main{% endif %}">
                                    <img src="{{ url }}" alt="Фото {{ forloop.counter }}">

                                    {% if forloop.first %}
                                        <span class="photo-main-badge">Основное</span>
                                    {% else %}
                                        <button type="submit"
                                                form="set-main-{{ forloop.counter0 }}"
                                                class="photo-main-btn"
                                                title="Сделать основным">★</button>
                                    {% endif %}

                                    <button type="submit"
                                            form="remove-photo-{{ forloop.counter0 }}"
                                            class="photo-remove-btn"
                                            title="Удалить"
                                            onclick="return confirm('Удалить это фото?');">×</button>
                                </div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                {% endif %}
            {% endwith %}
        {% endif %}

        <div class="form-actions">
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">
                {% if is_edit %}Сохранить{% else %}Опубликовать{% endif %}
            </button>
            {% if is_admin_edit %}
                <a href="{% url 'accounts:account_detail' %}?tab=admin-publications"
                   class="btn btn-secondary">Отмена</a>
            {% else %}
                <a href="{% url 'accounts:account_detail' %}?tab=publications"
                   class="btn btn-secondary">Отмена</a>
            {% endif %}
        </div>
    </form>

    {% if is_edit %}
        {% with urls=publication.image_urls %}
            {% for url in urls %}
                <form id="remove-photo-{{ forloop.counter0 }}"
                      method="post"
                      action="{% url 'core:publication_image_remove' publication.slug forloop.counter0 %}"
                      hidden>
                    {% csrf_token %}
                </form>
                <form id="set-main-{{ forloop.counter0 }}"
                      method="post"
                      action="{% url 'core:publication_image_set_main' publication.slug forloop.counter0 %}"
                      hidden>
                    {% csrf_token %}
                </form>
            {% endfor %}
        {% endwith %}
    {% endif %}
</section>
{% endblock %}

{% endblock %}

                </div>
            {% endif %}

            <div class="order-meta-row">
                <span class="order-meta-label">Заказчик</span>
                <span class="order-meta-value">{{ order.customer.username }}</span>
            </div>

            {% if order.executor %}
                <div class="order-meta-row">
                    <span class="order-meta-label">Исполнитель</span>
                    <span class="order-meta-value">{{ order.executor.username }}</span>
                </div>
            {% endif %}

            {% if order.publication %}
                <div class="order-meta-row">
                    <span class="order-meta-label">Публикация</span>
                    <a href="{% url 'core:publication_detail' order.publication.slug %}"
                       class="order-meta-value">
                        {{ order.publication.title }}
                    </a>
                </div>
            {% endif %}

            <div class="order-meta-row">
                <span class="order-meta-label">Создан</span>
                <span class="order-meta-value">{{ order.created_at|date:"d.m.Y H:i" }}</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    {% if order.description %}
        <div class="order-card">
            <h2>Описание</h2>
            <div class="order-description">
                {{ order.description|linebreaks }}
            </div>
        </div>
    {% endif %}

    <div class="order-actions">
        {# Кнопка "Оплатить" — через связанный Payment, а не сам Order #}
        {% if order.status == 'pending_payment' and request.user.id == order.customer_id and order.payment_id %}
            <form method="post" action="{% url 'orders:pay_payment' order.payment_id %}">
                {% csrf_token %}
                <button type="submit" class="order-btn order-btn-primary">
                    Оплатить {{ order.amount|floatformat:0 }} ₽
                </button>
            </form>
        {% endif %}

        {% if order.can_be_confirmed and request.user.id == order.customer_id %}
            <form method="post" action="{% url 'orders:confirm' order.id %}"
                  onsubmit="return confirm('Подтвердить выполнение заказа? Средства будут переведены исполнителю');">
                {% csrf_token %}
                <button type="submit" class="order-btn order-btn-primary">
                    Подтвердить выполнение
                </button>
            </form>
        {% endif %}

        {% if order.can_be_cancelled %}
            <form method="post" action="{% url 'orders:cancel' order.id %}"
                  onsubmit="return confirm('Отменить заказ? Если оплата была, средства вернутся заказчику.');">
                {% csrf_token %}
                <button type="submit" class="order-btn order-btn-danger">
                    Отменить заказ
                </button>
            </form>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    <div class="order-card">
        <h2>История транзакций</h2>

        {% if order.transactions.all %}
            <div class="order-transactions">
                {% for tx in order.transactions.all %}
                    <div class="tx-row">
                        <span class="tx-type">{{ tx.get_type_display }}</span>
                        <span class="tx-amount">{{ tx.amount|floatformat:0 }} ₽</span>
                        <span class="tx-status-{{ tx.status }}">{{ tx.get_status_display }}</span>
                        <span class="tx-date">{{ tx.created_at|date:"d.m.Y H:i" }}</span>
                    </div>
                {% endfor %}
            </div>
        {% else %}
            <p style="color: #A0AEC0; font-size: 14px;">Транзакций пока нет</p>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

</section>
{% endblock %}
